Document 001: 10月CPI再超预期，基本锁定12月美联储加息，升息幅度仍有不确定性

### Page 1

从环比细分数据来看，10 月核心商品和住房 CPI 通胀率均略微下滑，
但运输、医疗等服务价格仍有韧性，核心通胀降温仍不顺畅（图表 1）。

 核心商品价格环比通胀率回落 0.09 个百分点到 0.16%，二手车价
格转跌形成明显拖累，高油价传导效应开始显现。其中，运输 CPI
环比通胀率部分因为二手车价格大跌而走低 0.77 个百分点到-
0.38%。教育和通讯商品 CPI 环比通胀率亦收窄 0.37 个百分点到
0.5%，或受到计算机、外围设备 CPI 环比通胀率下滑的影响。这
或暗示着芯片价格暴涨引起的相关产业链价格大涨的最坏时期
已经过去。与此同时，8-9 月连续低迷的家具和医疗护理商品价格
10 月环比增速显著改善。家具价格的上升或与油价传导有关。娱
乐商品 CPI 环比通胀率略微下滑，但仍维持 0.71%的较高水平。
二手车涨价趋势的逆转表明，前期部分推升因素确有波动性，但
其他商品价格回升抵消了部分降温作用。核心商品尚未回到持续
通缩状态。

 住房 CPI 环比通胀率 10 月仅略微回落 0.09 个百分点到 0.3%，仍
是通胀重要来源。尽管涨幅有所收窄，住房仍贡献了超过三成的
当月核心 CPI 增幅，反映高权重分项的通胀粘性仍强。其中，最为
关键的业主等价租金 CPI 环比通胀率 10 月仅小幅下滑 0.03 个百
分点到 0.32%。主要居所租金 CPI 环比通胀率微降至 0.2%。但考虑
到房价趋势，我们依然认为该分项有下降空间，后续需观察租金
和业主等价租金 CPI 环比通胀率能否连续放缓。外宿 CPI 环比通
胀率从 9 月的 2.04%下滑到 0.64%。

 排除了住房的核心服务 CPI（超级核心服务 CPI）环比通胀率 10
月反弹 0.05 个百分点到 0.39%，压力仍较为突出。娱乐和运输服
务 CPI 环比通胀率的上升是主要推动力，两者均在 10 月升到 0.8%
以上的较高水平。细看运输服务分项，油价涨幅的收窄推动机票
价格环比增速下滑（10 月：0.4%，9 月：0.4%），但汽车保险价格
浦银国际 
数据点评 
10月CPI再超预期，基本锁定12月美联储加息，升息幅度仍有不确定性 
金晓雯，PhD，CFA

2026 年 11 月 11 日

### Page 2

10 月美国非农新增就业人数明显超预期，有助于减轻美联储对劳动力
市场走弱的顾虑。10 月非农新增就业 16.5 万人，远高于路透调查预期
的 6.5 万人，主要由专业和商业服务、医疗保健及运输仓储业拉动。8
月和 9 月新增就业分别上修至 18.6 万和 6.0 万人，合计上修 8.4 万人
（图表 2）。在劳动参与率小幅上升的情况下（上升 0.1 个百分点到
61.9%），失业率由 9 月的 4.174%略微下降至 4.141%，显示就业人数增
长快于劳动力人口（图表 3）。不过，因经济原因被迫兼职的人数增加，
广义失业率（U-6）由 7.6%升至 7.9%。与修订后的 9 月数据比较，私
营部门平均时薪环比增速由 0.3%加快至 0.4%，同比增速则由 3.2%微
降至 3.1%，工资趋势仍较温和。就业超预期减轻了美联储对劳动力市
场走弱的顾虑，但非农新增就业数据今年以来波动较大，我们认为并
不需要对单一数据过度解读。接下来，若财政刺激的边际提振减弱、
经济动能放缓，叠加 AI 应用压低部分岗位的招聘需求，我们认为就业
增长或仍逐步降温。

10 月继续超预期的核心 CPI 数据再次凸显核心通胀粘性，高油价传导
效应开始显现。二手车价格已经回落、住房价格涨幅亦放缓，但核心
CPI 连续第三个月环比上涨 0.3%，说明基础价格压力尚未明显消退。
此外，此前仅主要反映在机票 CPI 的高油价传导效应 10 月开始向家
具、城际交通 CPI 等分项传导。不过，各分项走势仍有分化，单月数据
尚不足以确认全面再通胀。

接下来，油价回落有望缓和后续整体通胀，但核心通胀回落仍可能偏
慢。特朗普 11 月 9 日表示暂缓进一步对伊朗军事打击，但仍维持海上
封锁，美伊尚未达成最终协议。布伦特原油期货 11 月 10 日盘中一度
跌破每桶 90 美元，较 10 月底高点明显回落。若局势继续缓和、油价
维持较低水平，汽油等能源分项对通胀的推升作用有望进一步减弱。
然而，美联储 11 月 9 日发布的研究显示，疫情后油价向核心通胀的传
导有所增强，前期高能源成本仍可能滞后影响商品与服务定价，这一
点在 10 月数据中已有所体现。与此同时，AI 数据中心扩建可能加剧局
部电力和施工资源约束，尽管民主党赢得国会控制权以及部分关键州
州长竞选或对数据中心的建设形成一定阻力。不过，9 月实际可支配
收入环比下降 0.3%、实际消费近乎持平，家庭购买力承压或限制企业
向消费者转嫁成本的空间。

### Page 3

我们维持 12 月美联储加息 25 个基点的基准判断，但单次加息 50 个
基点的风险正在上升。10 月美联储会议表明通胀仍高、且为追加紧缩
保留依据，但沃什对政策已开始见效的评价增加了观望空间。从基本
面来看，10 月就业超预期及此前月份数据上修缓解了美联储对劳动力
市场走弱的顾虑，核心 CPI 连续三个月上涨 0.3%则强化了追加紧缩的
理由。美联储理事沃勒 11 月 10 日在美国 CPI 数据公布前表示，若核
心 CPI 环比仍在 0.3%或更高，应考虑 12 月加息 50 个基点。本次核心
CPI 读数达到其提出的条件，但这一表态仍属沃勒个人观点，不能等同
于 FOMC 共识。考虑到近期油价明显回落、住房和核心商品涨幅放缓，
我们仍维持 12 月美联储再加息 25 个基点的基本判断，但由于超预期
的 10 月通胀和就业数据，12 月单次加息 50 个基点的风险也明显上
升，后续需关注其他官员是否跟进支持，以及 11 月非农就业数据、10
月 PCE 通胀数据发布和通胀预期变化。11 月 CPI 通胀数据发布时间将
晚于 12 月 8-9 日召开的 12 月议息会议。对于明年政策路径，需重点
关注 12 月经济预测及利率预测的调整，以及潜在的 5 个工作小组的报
告，后者可能也会影响美联储对利率路径的判断。目前来看，我们仍
倾向于认为美联储本轮是预防性加息，而非过热性加息。

在中期选举的影响问题上，我们认为国会控制权易手对美联储短期政
策的影响或有限。民主党控制两院减少了一项潜在的额外需求刺激，这
有助于降低进一步加息的尾部风险。此外，当前更为制衡的美国政治环
境亦有可能放缓美国 AI 投资发展，从而缓解从 AI 投资扩张带来的价格
压力。不过，具体的货币政策走势仍依赖于通胀和就业之间的平衡。

另外，联邦法官、内阁及高级官员任命由参议院确认。在民主党控制
参议院的情况下，美联储理事的任命或受阻（地区联储行长不经参议
院确认）。未来任何美联储理事会的提名都有可能遭到冻结。然而，目
前七名理事席位均已满，沃什也已就任主席，短期内并不会自然形成
理事会席位长期空缺。只有在 2027 年后席位出现空缺，民主党掌控下
的参议院才可通过延迟确认来限制特朗普继续调整理事会构成（参见：
民主党在美国中期选举中大获全胜，将如何影响美国经济和金融市
场？）。

风险提示： 通胀上行风险包括美伊局势反复推升油价，前期能源成本
向核心商品和服务价格的传导强于预期，以及 AI 投资加剧电力、原材
料和施工资源供需紧张，导致核心通胀粘性超预期，美联储加息幅度
或后续紧缩力度超过基准判断。下行风险包括美伊局势持续缓和、油
价回落快于预期，住房通胀降温加快，以及就业、消费或 AI 投资走弱
超预期，促使通胀更快回落并降低进一步加息的必要性。

### Page 6

本报告之收取者透过接受本报告（包括任何有关的附件），表示及保证其根据下述的条件下有权获得本报告，且同意受
此中包含的限制条件所约束。任何没有遵循这些限制的情况可能构成法律之违反。

本报告是由从事证券及期货条例(香港法例第 571 章)中第一类(证券交易)及第四类(就证券提供意见)受规管活动之持

自被认为是可靠的来源，但并不保证数据之准确性、可信性及完整性，亦不会因资料引致的任何损失承担任何责任。
报告中的资料来源除非另有说明，否则信息均来自本集团。本报告的内容涉及到保密数据，所以仅供阁下为其自身利

本报告之观点、推荐、建议和意见均不一定反映浦银国际证券的立场。浦银国际控股有限公司及其联属公司、关联公

米国：AI関連を中心に製造業生産は堅調も、資材・設備の需給ひっ迫が他産業に波及
 米国の製造業生産は、AI関連投資を背景にコンピューター・電子製品、一次金属、一般機械などが堅調
― 一方、非耐久財や家具など消費関連の生産は軟調に推移し、業種間のばらつきが拡大
 FRBは、データセンター建設に伴う鋼材・電気設備などの需要増加が、他の建設案件や生産活動に影響していると指摘
―AI投資の拡大が関連産業の生産を押し上げる一方、供給制約を通じて他産業の活動を圧迫する兆候も
― 当面はAI投資による成長押し上げ効果が期待される一方、供給制約や価格上昇を通じた負の影響にも注意が必要

日本：全国コアCPIは再び2％超え。基調的なサービス価格も伸び高まる
5
 9月の全国コアCPIは前年比＋2.1％（前月：同＋1.7％）と加速。2025年12月以来の2％超に
― 原油高・円安等のコスト上昇もあり、家事用消耗品や教養娯楽用品等を中心に伸び率が拡大
― 一般サービス価格も伸び加速。シルバーウィークの並びによる宿泊料や外食費の上昇などが主因
 特殊要因を除いた一般サービス価格もじわり上昇
― 変動の大きい宿泊費や私立高校無償化による押し下げを除いた基調的な一般サービス価格に上昇圧力。人件費増の
価格転嫁が進んでいる可能性を示唆

 連合は、2027年春闘賃上げ要求の目安を「6％中心、5％以上」と発表
―5％以上の賃上げを最低目標として明確化。実質賃金1％上昇の定着をめざす方針
― 中小企業は大企業との格差是正に向け「6％以上」が目標。過去4年のインフレで実質賃金が低下した分の回復も要求
― また、新たに、2030年に2026年の賃金水準を15％程度上回る中期目標を設定。持続的な賃金上昇をめざす
 賃上げ余力は企業規模・業種で濃淡。中小企業等で高い「賃上げ5％の壁」
― なお、6％に近い賃上げが実現する場合、サービス価格への波及が強まり、基調的物価上昇率が2％を上回る可能性

MSCI 降評風險升高，印尼股市保守看待
2026 年 10 月 27 日
一、 市場動態
1. MSCI 擬將印尼降至獨立市場，降評風險明顯升高：MSCI 於 10 月 15 日宣布，啟動
將印尼股市由「新興市場（Emerging Market）」 直接降至「獨立市場（Standalone
Market）」的諮詢程序，意見徵詢將於 2027 年 4 月 30 日截止，最晚於 2027 年 6 月
30 日公布最終決定。MSCI 原先於今年 1 月提出可能降至「邊境市場（Frontier Market
）」，此次則跳過「邊境市場」提出直接將該國股市降至「獨立市場」的方案，顯示對

印尼市場治理的疑慮進一步加深。雖然「邊境市場」仍可能是替代分類方案，但 MSCI
強調，除非有明確證據顯示市場治理及運作出現實質改善，否則預期將依目前提案重
新分類，顯示此次降評風險較過去顯著升高。
2. 股權透明度與交易秩序問題為 MSCI 主要疑慮：MSCI 指出，印尼股市長期存在股權
結構不透明、實際自由流通股比例難以確認、疑似協調交易，以及市場監理執法成效
不足等問題，影響國際投資人對市場公平性與可投資性的信心。雖然印尼主管機關已
提出強化持股超過 1%股東資訊揭露、建立高持股集中度（HSC）監理機制，以及規
劃逐步將最低自由流通股比例提高至 15%等改革措施，但 MSCI 認為相關改革尚未展
現持續且具實質性的改善成果。由於問題涉及市場制度與治理結構，改善所需時間可
能較一般短期市場波動更長。
3. 股市反彈後再度回落，外資撤離壓力仍高：受降評消息衝擊，印尼雅加達綜合指數 10
月 16 日重挫 11.57%，創 1998 年以來最大單日跌幅，19 日再跌 3.64%，盤中最低

觸及 4,907.37 點。雖然 20 日至 23 日連續四個交易日反彈，但 26 日再度下跌 1.22%
，收於 5,373.09 點，各產業類股全面收黑，顯示市場信心仍不穩定。根據 Reuters
報導，截至 10 月 19 日，2026 年外資已累計淨賣超印尼股票約 48.8 億美元。此外，
印尼證交所 26 日宣布，將引進獨立專家及外國投資人代表參與高持股集中度審查，
並自 11 月起加強調查疑似協調交易，必要時凍結可疑帳戶，以回應 MSCI 對市場制
度的疑慮。
二、 下跌原因分析：
1. 股市：潛在指數剔除與資金外流，持續壓抑市場評價
若印尼股市最終降至「獨立市場」，原則上將不再納入 MSCI 新興市場及邊境市場相關
指數，追蹤相關指數的被動式基金將須依規則調整持股，實際處理方式仍以 MSCI 後
續公告為準。若改降至「邊境市場」，則可能獲得部分邊境市場資金承接，衝擊相對較
小。Reuters 引述多家金融機構估計，印尼股市降至獨立市場可能引發約 30 億至 60

### Page 2

確性不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決
策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元
大銀行所有，非經本行同意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕
不另行通知。
億美元被動資金流出，其中 UBS 估計約 40 億美元，並指出主動型基金另有約 150 億
美元潛在撤資風險，惟實際規模仍視最終分類結果及基金策略而定。
2. 匯率：外資撤離增加印尼盾貶值壓力
印尼股市的降評風險不僅衝擊股市，也可能透過外資撤離增加印尼盾貶值壓力。印尼
盾 10 月 16 日盤中一度貶至 18,070 兌 1 美元，為 7 月下旬以來最弱水準之一，當日
收盤約 18,000。若後續國際資金持續流出，可能進一步增加股匯市波動，對以外幣計
價、且未完全避險的印尼基金及 ETF 形成額外壓力。雖然印尼央行可透過匯市干預及

流動性管理措施穩定金融市場，但在資金外流壓力尚未緩解前，印尼盾仍可能維持偏
弱格局。
3. 基金與 ETF：指數權重下滑，單一市場曝險風險升高
MSCI 相關指數調整已對印尼股票產生實質影響。Reuters 引用 LSEG 資料顯示，印尼
在 MSCI 新興市場指數的權重已由 2026 年初的 1.16%降至 9 月底的 0.43%，相關成
分股亦由年初 18 檔減至 9 檔，反映部分指數資金調整壓力已提前出現。對印尼單一
國家基金及 ETF 而言，主要風險來自股市下跌、資金流出及印尼盾波動；主動式基金
雖可透過選股降低部分影響，但仍難以完全迴避整體市場風險。不同產品實際受到的
影響，仍應依追蹤指數、主要持股及匯率避險安排個別評估。
三、 市場看法：
1. 制度性問題難以速解，降評風險將持續牽動市場：我們認為，此次 MSCI 降評風險並
非單純反映經濟成長放緩或企業獲利不佳，而是涉及市場透明度、交易公平性與監理
執法等結構性問題。尤其 MSCI 不僅啟動降評諮詢，更持續限制印尼股票的部分指數

調整，包括不提高外資納入因子、不新增相關成分股，以及不進行向上規模分類調整
，顯示市場可投資性疑慮尚未獲得有效解決。儘管政府試圖推動改革，但制度修正仍
須經過執行與成效驗證，才能重新取得國際投資人的信任，因此短期內降評風險仍難
完全消除。
2. 外資撤離與指數權重下降，市場信心修復恐需較長時間：印尼今年以來外資已大幅賣
超，MSCI 新興市場指數中的權重亦明顯下降，顯示國際投資人對當地市場的疑慮已
逐漸反映於實際資金流向。我們認為，即使印尼最終成功避免降評，部分國際機構投
資人仍可能因市場透明度、流動性及治理品質疑慮而維持較低曝險。此外，MSCI 對
部分指數調整的凍結措施尚未解除，短期內新增指數資金流入機會仍受到限制，預期
國際資金回流與市場評價修復均可能需要較長時間。
3. 監理改革力度加大，但尚不足以扭轉保守展望：印尼政府近期持續加強改革，包括提
高資訊透明度、強化交易監控、對違規交易開罰，以及引進外部獨立人士參與監理審
查。10 月 26 日該國政府進一步提出主動調查疑似協調交易及凍結可疑帳戶等措施，

11 月 2 日起亦規劃將高持股集中度股票排除於雅加達綜合指數之外。相關改革有助改

### Page 3

確性不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決
策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元
大銀行所有，非經本行同意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕
不另行通知。
善交易秩序及短期市場信心，但部分措施仍待正式實施，且實際成效尚未獲得 MSCI
認可。考量制度性問題改善需要時間，政策利多雖可能帶動短線反彈，但尚不足以改
變對印尼股市中期前景的保守判斷。
4. 股市跌深不代表利空出盡，中期風險報酬吸引力偏低：印尼股市在 10 月 16 日及 19
日大幅修正後，雖曾連續四個交易日反彈，但 26 日再度回落，顯示市場尚未形成穩
定回升趨勢。我們認為，當前股市面臨的不僅是短期賣壓，更包括國際投資人信任下
降及風險溢價提高，即使企業基本面未同步惡化，仍可能因評價下修而承受股價壓力

。因此，短線不排除政策利多或跌深後出現技術性反彈，但在 MSCI 態度、外資流向
與印尼盾走勢明顯改善前，反彈延續性仍有疑慮，印尼股市中期展望維持保守。
四、市場展望：
1. 印尼股市維持保守展望，單一市場風險報酬吸引力不足：考量 MSCI 降評風險尚未解除、
外資撤離壓力持續，以及市場治理改革仍有待驗證，我們認為印尼股市未來數季的風險報
酬吸引力相對有限。即使近期股價明顯回落，仍難認定相關風險已充分反映。相較市場制
度成熟、國際資金流動性較佳的新興市場，印尼股市可能持續面臨較高風險溢價，因此對
單一印尼市場的集中曝險應維持審慎態度。
2. 此事件對新興市場指數與東協指數影響相對有限：根據 MSCI 資料，截至 2026 年 9 月底
，印尼股市在 MSCI Emerging Market 指數與 MSCI AC ASEAN 指數中的權重分別約為
0.43%與 7.55%，因此我們認為印尼潛在降評對相關指數的直接衝擊相對有限。此外，新
興市場各國經濟結構與產業組成有所差異，區域型基金可透過跨國分散降低單一市場風險

。惟後續仍須留意印尼事件對整體新興市場投資信心造成的外溢影響，以及全球貿易與匯
率波動風險。

評等分級：買進（預期未來六個月股價漲幅超過+30%）; 逢低買進（預期未來六個月股價漲幅介於+15%與+30%之間）; 區間操

前次投資建議：2026.08.05 
「買進」，目標價: $ 6945

報告日期：2026.11.09 研 究 員：邱岳霖

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ 高階材料放量、報價持續上揚、新產能開出，上修 2026年 EPS至 132元 
➢ 2027年高階材料規格升級、產能持續擴增， EPS 280.8 元，考量公司在產業具有主導地位
且未來幾年將持續高速增長，不過因利率走升與市場面臨短期逆風，故下修估值，兆豐投顧
降低目標價至 6,460元(23X 27(F)PER)，潛在上漲空間 69.1%，投資評等維持「買進」。

基本資料 結論與建議

+222.9%，EPS 132.0元。

- 2 - 
個股報告 
資料來源：CMoney；兆豐投顧預估

營 運 分 析

台光電為全球銅箔基板龍頭廠  
台光電成立於 1992年並於 1998年市，總部位於桃園觀音，為全球最大無鹵
素銅箔基板供應商，AI Server 中 CCL 市占率超過 50%，同時是 HDI 材料
龍頭。主力產品為銅箔基板 (CCL)、黏合片 (PP)及多層壓合板 (Mass 
Lamination)，不過自 2020年起切入 IC載板材料(BT/ABF)之 CCL以作為第
二成長曲線，目前正積極推進客戶認證與產能擴充。

圖一、台光電為全球 CCL與 HDI材料龍頭廠商

資料來源：公司；兆豐投顧整理

生產基地包含台灣桃園觀音、新竹，中國江蘇昆山、廣東中山、湖北黃石，
以及馬來西亞檳城。銅箔基板月產能現況約 585 萬張，預計 2026 年底增至
約 615萬張，2027年底目標達 900萬張，且近期已與客戶敲定至 2030年產
能至 1,525 萬張。26Q3 以基礎設施應用(AI 伺服器、Switch、低軌衛星等)
為主，占比約 80%。

圖二、台光電全球布局與產能規劃

報價上漲紅利，M9 新產品出貨，營收與毛利率雙創高，Q3 EPS 40.5 元 
台光電(2383)公告 26Q3 財報：營收 608.85 億元，QoQ +28.8%/YoY 
+142.1%，再創歷史新高，係因報價上揚，同時 M9 開始出貨帶動所致，毛
利率 37.7%，季增 3.8ppt，年增 7.6ppt，大幅優於研究部預期之 35%，受惠
報價上漲完整反映，同時產品組合轉佳帶動，不過因持續投入新世代產品研
發帶動整體營業費用較上季增長逾 30%，稅後淨利 145 億元，QoQ 
+46.9%/YoY +265.7%，EPS 40.5元，遠優於預期之 33.5元。

- 4 - 
個股報告 
高階材料放量、報價持續上揚、新產能開出，上修 2026年 EPS至 132元 
展望後市，隨著 AI Server、高速交換器與 General server 需求持續強勁，公
司持續擴產以滿足市場需求，月產能預計 2026 年底增至約 615 萬張，季增
約 5%，此外由於 AI Server 規格持續提升，高階材料佔比將逐步墊高，同時
BT 材料已開始認證，預期年底有機會開始放量出貨；原物料方面，研究部認
為高階 CCL缺料格局將延續，同時反映原物料成本上升，預期報價有望再上
調 5-20%，此外更高階材料 M9 於 26Q3 開始出貨，預期 Q4 佔比將持續擴
大。

在整體產品組合轉佳與漲價效應帶動下，同時考量 Q3 財報遠優研究部預
期，故上修台光電26Q4營收至 700.2億元(原估593億元)，QoQ +15.0%/YoY 
+180.9%，毛利率 39%(原估 35.5%)，季增 1.3ppt，年增 10.4ppt，稅後淨
利 175.9億元，QoQ +21.3%/YoY +370.7%，EPS 49.1元(原估 37.2 元)；
同步上修 2026年稅後淨利 473億元，YoY +222.9%，EPS 132元(原估 113.2
元)。

2027年高階材料規格升級、產能持續擴增， EPS 280.8 元 
展望 2027 年，隨著 GPU 與 Switch 規格持續提升，帶動 PCB/CCL 層數與
尺寸持續提升，且 CSP持續擴大 Capex，ASIC需求亦繼續上調，目前公司
訂單能見度已達 2027 年，亦積極擴張產能，預期至 27 年底達月產能 900
萬張目標，年增近 50%。而客戶主動要求簽 LTA 並確保後續產能無虞，目
前台光電已通過至 2030 年產能建置計畫，預期將總產能大幅增加至 1,525
萬張，為現有產能的 2.5 倍，將有助於公司未來持續高速增長；此外 BT 載
板材料預計 26年底完成認證，27年開始出貨，初步規劃出貨量約 10萬張，
不過為確保未來高速成長的需求，將規劃 120萬張產能，預期將帶來新一波
成長動能。綜上所述，考量整體市場供需格局持續偏緊，且公司產能擴充積
極，輔以報價持續上升，兆豐投顧上修台光電 2027年營收至 3697.2 億元(原
估 3,209.5 億元)，YoY +75.0%，而毛利率方面，預期在新一代 GPU 推出，
同時 Switch 亦升級至 3.2T 下，M9 材料營收佔比可望由今年低個位數提升
至雙位數，在稼動率維持高檔、產品組合持續優化下，上修毛利率至 41.2%(原
估 36.6%)，年增 5.2ppt，稅後淨利 1006.2億元，YoY +112.7%，EPS 280.8
元(原估 210元)。

### Page 5

評等分級：買進（預期未來六個月股價漲幅超過+30%）; 逢低買進（預期未來六個月股價漲幅介於+15%與+30%之間）; 區間操
作（預期未來六個月股價漲跌幅介於-15%與+15%之間）; 逢高賣出（預期未來六個月股價跌幅介於-15%與-30%之間）；賣出（預

### Page 1

M
Update
Novatek  | Asia Pacific
Consumer Demand Softens as
Cost Pressure Builds
3Q growth led by smartphones: Revenue was NT$30.97bn, +8% Q/Q and +26% Y/
Y, 7% above MSe. Net income was NT$6.08bn, -4% Q/Q and +66% Y/Y, 2% above
MSe, with EPS of NT$9.99. Smartphone restocking lifted sales, but GM fell 1.53ppt
Q/Q to 40.89% on a lower SoC mix and a lag in cost pass-through. Lower opex
supported a 9.6% Q/Q rise in operating profit; net income declined in the absence of

2Q's NT$860mn one-off investment gain.
4Q revenue and margins seen declining sequentially: Management guided for
revenue of NT$28.0-29.2bn, down 6-10% Q/Q, with GM of 37-39% and OpM of 17-
19%, assuming NT$/US$32. Large DDICs should face the largest sequential revenue
drop, particularly from notebooks, followed by SoC; small/medium DDICs should
decrease slightly despite stronger Chinese OLED handset orders. Lower-cost
inventory depletion, product mix and higher memory KGD costs will likely weigh on

margins. Prices have been adjusted across SoC and DDIC products, but management
still sees higher memory prices constraining consumer demand into 2027.
New products offer growth, with cost risks unresolved: Automotive eDP TDDI is
scheduled for mass production in 4Q26, while touch-enabled notebook products are
scheduled to ramp through 2027. OLED solutions for IT applications and higher-
performance handset products broaden opportunities, although smartphone OLED

DDIC shipments are expected to decline Y/Y in 2026. Management expects new
products to support 2027 revenue growth but provided no quantified target or
assurance of flat Y/Y margins. Further foundry and OSAT price increases remain
under negotiation, leaving earnings dependent upon product mix and the pace of
cost pass-through.
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+
Daniel Yen, CFA
Equity Analyst

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Novatek (3034.TW)
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions: cost of equity constant at 9.8% (2.0%
risk-free rate, 6% risk premium, 1.3 beta), payout ratio of 80%, medium-term growth rate of
6.1% and terminal growth rate assumption of 3.4%.
Risks to Upside
n Faster-than-expected 8K/4K TV growth.
n Better-than-expected smartphone sell-through.
n Better-than-expected sell-through of foldable products.

Risks to Downside
n Intensified price competition in TDDI, driving Novatek's gross margin below its corpo-
rate average.
n Slower-than-expected 8K/4K TV growth.
n Slower sell-through of foldable products.

In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

King Slide

Buy: strong 3Q26 results, 88.2% GM; solid 
’27/28E sales/margin; cut PO to NT$12,988

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 12,988 TWD | Price: 8,165 TWD

3Q26 results: another impressive beat with 88.2% GM 
King Slide’s 3Q26 GM/OPM hit record highs of 88.2%/82.9% for the fourth/second 
consecutive quarter, respectively, well above BofAe of 83.7%/78.2% and consensus of 
84.0%/78.6%. OP (+62% QoQ, +383% YoY) was 7%/5% above BofAe/consensus. We 
attribute the solid margins/results to sizable sales scale (+60% QoQ, +297% YoY) and 
better operating efficiency. With non-operating gain (mainly net interest income), NP 
(+65% QoQ, +266% YoY) was 8%/7% above BofAe/consensus (Exhibit 3). 
BofAe 4Q26 sales -20% QoQ; solid 2027/28 sales/margin 
We now expect King Slide’s 4Q26 sales to decline 20% QoQ, from the high 3Q26 base 
and amid GPU platform transition. We believe Sep/Oct sales (-31%/+2% MoM) shall 
serve as evidence for slower order momentum. Our 4Q26 sales forecast implies flat-to-
slightly higher MoM sales in Nov/Dec. We now expect another 45%/20% YoY sales 
growth in 2027/28, with stronger pull-in demand resuming in 1Q27. In our view, 
2027/28 sales should be supported by various AI projects across platforms (GPU/ASIC/ 
AMD) and clients (global/regional CSPs, neoclouds, AI start-ups, enterprises). We also 
keep monitoring the capacity ramp-up from the new US Houston site, which may bring 
further sales upside. We expect high and sustainable GM at 85.0% both in 2027/28. We 
see FX as a potential swing factor to GM, given the company’s natural-hedging policy.  
Trim PO to NT$12,988: lower P/E set-up; reiterate Buy 
We lower our 2026-27E EPS by 0-4% but lift our 2028E EPS by 7%, mainly to reflect 
3Q26 beat and stronger margins. Yet, we cut PO to NT$12,988 (28x 2027E EPS) from 
NT$14,888 (32x 2027E EPS), with valuation base unchanged but a lower P/E multiple. 
We now use 28x (2SD above average since IPO) on valuation de-rating across AI supply 
chain amid macro uncertainty. Nevertheless, we reiterate Buy on King Slide in view of its 
lion share in AI server rail market, diversified client/project base, strong margin profile.

Company Sector 
IT Hardware

Company Description 
King Slide is the global top server rail kit manufacturer from 
Taiwan. It also supplies slide rail kit for kitchen/furniture. Its 
key server clients include global cloud service providers and 
enterprise servers.

King Slide was founded in 1986 and was publicly listed on 
Taiwan Stock Exchange on 25 June 2008. Its headquarter 
and main production sites are located in Kaohsiung 
(Taiwan). It also has capacity in Houston (US).

Investment Rationale 
We assign a Buy rating to King Slide given its leading 
position in server rail to ride on strong AI market demand 
with rising content value thanks to increasing component 
density and customization. We highlight King Slide's early 
step into server market since 2001 with proven track 
record, much larger scale, time-to-market capability, higher 
automation level, and 3,600+ patents as strong moats for 
high/above-peer margins.

### Page 6

Price objective basis & risk 
King Slide (KGSLF) 
Our PO of NT$12,988 is based on 28x 2027E EPS. We use 28x P/E multiple (2SD above 
P/E average since listing) in view of strong AI server market demand, leading position in 
server rail, solid/high margin profiles, and double-digit sales/EPS CAGR in 2026-28E. Its 
early step into server market since 2001 with proven track record, much larger scale 
than peer, and 3,600+ patents as strong moats for high/above-peer margins.

Upside risks to our PO are: (1) even stronger AI server demand, (2) margin upside thanks 
to larger scale and/or better efficiency, (3) better kitchen slides market demand, (4) 
faster capacity expansion, (5) favorable FX movement (mainly USD/NTD) for GM and/or 
non-operating items.

Downside risks to our PO are: (1) softer AI server demand due to GPU/components 
shortage, (2) GM pressure due to higher raw material cost (e.g. steel, aluminum), (3) 
softer kitchen slides market demand under macro uncertainty/weakness, (4) delayed 
product certification, (5) slower capacity expansion, (6) unfavorable FX movement 
(mainly USD/NTD) to weigh on GM and/or non-operating items.

### Page 1

M
Update
Compal Electronics  | Asia Pacific
Server Ramp Takes Shape but
Valuation Keeps Us UW
Compal’s server expansion is progressing, with revenue
contribution rising to ~12% in 3Q and management targeting 30-
40% in CY27. However, weaker PC volumes and diminishing
scale benefits continue to pressure margins. At ~14x CY27E P/E,
valuation remains stretched.
3Q OpM missed. See our  3Q26 Results Summary                   .
PC business facing demand and profitability headwinds: Compal expects a mid-

teens q/q decline in 4Q26 shipments because of higher selling prices. This weakness
should persist into CY27; the company projects a likely double-digit y/y unit decline,
though it admitted that visibility remains low. However, rising ASPs from mix
changes and cost pass-through could keep 4Q PC revenue up sequentially.
Servers have become a key growth driver: Servers rose from ~5% of the mix in 1Q
to ~12% in 3Q, with a CY27 revenue contribution target of 30-40%. While 4Q26

server revenue is guided to be flat q/q, Compal is aggressively scaling its capacity. It
now primarily ships from Taiwan, and a major L10/11 ramp will begin in 2Q27 at its
new Texas plant. It currently does L10 HGX for Neocloud customers and L6 for a US
enterprise customer; it will do L10/11 for enterprise customer(s) as well, and is
actively approaching CSP customers for ASIC L11 solutions as well.
Margins to remain pressured: Overall margins were pressured by the PC segment,

driving a sequential decline in 3Q owing to lower PC volumes and utilization. This
margin headwind should intensify in 4Q because of shrinking PC business scale.
Conversely, server gross margin should remain resilient in the high single digits to
10%,  staying flat q/q.
4Q preview: We estimate that 4Q revenue will grow ~8% q/q, to NT$263bn (+38%
y/y), primarily driven by higher PC ASPs despite declining volumes. GM should
contracting ~10bps q/q, to 4.3% (-150bps y/y), with declining PC profitability, mainly

owing to diminishing scale benefits. This leads to our OP estimate of NT$2.8bn
(+25% q/q, +6% y/y) and NP estimate of NT$1.7bn (-34% q/q, +21% y/y), for EPS of
NT$0.39. Our 4Q net profit estimate is down q/q because of lower non-op income.
Stay UW with price target unchanged at NT$23.0 (10.4x CY27 P/E). We think
valuation looks quite stretched now at ~14x CY27e P/E. We prefer other ODMs like
Wistron (6x), Hon Hai (12x), Wiwynn (8x), or Quanta (9x).

Morgan Stanley appreciates your support in
the 2027 Extel Asia Research Survey.

3Q26 Results Summary
3Q26 gross margin miss:
• Revenue was NT$243bn (+2% q/q, +30% y/y), and GM was 4.4% (-30bps q/q, -
130bps y/y), 80bps and 40bps below our estimate and the Street, respectively,
driven by lower PC margins.
• As a result, OP was NT$2.bn (-21% q/q, -16% y/y), 33% below our estimate/34%
be;low the Street, for OpM of 0.9% (-30bps q/q, -50bps y/y).
• Non-op profit was higher than expected, so net profit reached Nt$2.6bn (-18% q/q,

+33% y/y), 15% above our estimate/2% above the Street, for EPS of NT$0.59.

### Page 4

Update
4
Price Target and Valuation Methodology
Our 12-month price target is unchanged at  NT$23.0: Our price target is our base case
scenario value, derived from a multistage residual income (RI) model. We use the
company's beginning equity plus the present value of all expected residual income in
excess of the cost of capital. The RI is positive when ROAE is above the cost of capital and
negative when it is below.
We assume a cost of equity of 9.1%  (beta of 1.3, equity risk premium of 6.0% and risk-free

rate of 1.5%), a medium-term growth rate of 5%, and a terminal growth rate of 3% (all
unchanged).
Our new price target implies a 2026e P/E of 10.4x and a 2027e P/E of 8.5x, below the five-
year average of 13.8x. We view these as fair, because we factor in more conservative
assumptions for its consumer business and a weaker margin outlook for Compal. The
company's venture into the "new businesses" (e.g., auto, server, etc.) is constraining its
margin profile as it tries to shift from 80%+ PC to more commercial exposure.

Our bull and bear case values are also unchanged, at NT$38.0 and NT$17.0,
respectively.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Hon Hai Precision (2317.TW)
Base case scenario value, derived from a residual income valuation model. Our key assump-
tions include a cost of equity of 8.5%, a medium-term growth rate of 13%, and a terminal
growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected iPhone sell-through
n Faster progress in AI server business
n Faster EV business development progress
n Any new M&A activity that could improve sentiment

Risks to Downside
n Lower iPhone sell-through
n Smaller profit contribution from AI business
n Slower EV business development progress
n Geopolitical developments that could negatively affect foreign investment
Quanta Computer Inc. (2382.TW)
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions include a cost of equity of 9.0% (beta of
1.2, equity premium of 6.0% and risk-free rate of 1.5%), a 7.0% medium-term growth rate, and
a 3% terminal growth rate.

Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected NB demand

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

2026/11/09 投資速報

電子–半導體 
世芯-KY (3661.TT) 
破除市場謠言，27 年獲利上修

強力買進(調升評等) 
出刊緣由：財報公佈 
與前次比較： 上次 本次 
評 等 買進 強力買進 
目標價 5370 6850 
EPS 178.99(27EPS) 341.95(28EPS) 
收盤價 4060.00 2830.00 
大盤指數 45857.27 39701.64 
前次評等日期:2026/08/17 
市值(億) 2442.3 
股本(億) 8.630

淨負債/股東權益 48.54 
董監持股比率 2.27 
外資持股比率 48.11 
投信持股比率 5.29 
個股與大盤走勢

近一個月 近三個月 近半年 近一年 
加權指數報酬率 -20.3 -10.6 -3.5 42.3 
個股股價報酬率 -32.3 -22.1 -37.5 -22.3

營收走勢圖

研究員/吳大任 TERRY.WU@uni-psg.com

研究部調升世芯-KY 的投資建議為強力買進，目標價上修至 6850
元，基於以下五點理由：(1)3Q26 雖受到供應鏈緊張影響，營收仍
持續創高，(2)4Q26 營收將持續攀升，(3)27 年 CSP 3 代晶片貢獻
營收幅度上修，(4)新增 AI 晶片客戶，有望在 27 年下半年貢獻約
10 億美元營收，(5)CSP 4 代晶片更加確立，28 年營收更高層級的
營收，且費用控管得宜，OPM 純度持續增加。綜合以上，維持 26 年
EPS 預估為 120.31 元(原估120.3 元，+65.5%YOY)，上修 27 年 EPS
預估為 198.3 元(原估 178.99 元，+64.8%YOY)，及預估 28 年 EPS
為 341.95 元(+72.5%YOY)。雖然 1H26 營運動能表現不佳，但最差
狀況確實已過，目前供應鏈因素逐步排除，且在 27 年 CSP 客戶的
訂單配比增加，且已經證明世芯為北美 CSP 客戶唯一後段設計服務
供應商的角色，新增客戶產品也將在 27 年貢獻，先前股價修正幅
度較大，故上修建議至強力買進，目標價上修至 6850 元(27 年
EPS*35XPER OR 28 年EPS*20XPER)。

重點摘要：

1. 3Q26 受到供應鏈影響，惟營收仍維持高檔：  
世芯-KY 3Q26 營收為 261.9 億元(原估 284.5 億元)，成長
242.8%QOQ，成長 298.7%YOY，小幅不如原先預期主要係因
載板及封裝產能稀缺等負面影響，如產能有爭取夠將有望
增加約 1 億美元之營收，就與先前預估相當。毛利率下滑
至 17.95%，與原先預估 17.5%相差不大，主要是部分 NRE
有遞延認列影響。費用 10.7 億元，稍微高於於預估，不過
整體稅後淨利仍達到 32.51 億元(原估 33.09 億元)，成長
98.5%QOQ，成長 145%YOY，EPS 37.67 元(原估 39.28 元)，
差異來自於營收規模。

2. 4Q26 營收將續創新高： 
展望 4Q26，雖然在法說會上公司未提及財測數字預估，不
過 CSP 客戶 3nm 案件持續放量，先前有供應端材料不順的
影響，與供應商及客戶偕同持續排除下，推估營收有望續
創新高。故研究部維持 4Q26 營收預估為 334.5 億元，成長
27.7%QOQ，成長 607.6%YOY，毛利率與先前預估相當的
17.5%，稅後淨利預估為 40.66 億元(原估 39.1 億元)，成
長 25.1%QOQ，成長 174.2%YOY，EPS 47.11 元(原估 45.35
元)。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

3. CSP 3 代晶片推估 26 年貢獻 15 億美金，27 年確定上修 
法說會雖然因法規及與客戶 NDA 的關係，皆無提到 CSP 客
戶三代晶片能夠貢獻的營收幅度，不過從營收可以看出，
先前規劃的投片持續順利產出，26 年維持貢獻 15 億美元
營收不變。展望 27 年，先前預估較為保守，主要係因 3nm
產能稀缺，但此次法說會公司表示，在研發上持續配合客
戶，而客戶依賴度提升狀況下，27 年的訂單配比有所提升，
產能也有所確保，故上修成長幅度至 100%YOY 以上，先前
僅預估 60-70%YOY 左右。

4. CSP 4 代晶片訂單更加確立，且新增另一客戶： 
另外在 4 代晶片方面，法說會當中有提到 2nm 案件將在
4Q26 TAPE OUT，且再次宣告也只有一家後段設計服務供
應商，包含 IO DIE 及連結部分，且公司也扮演整合晶片的
角色，以時間點來看，將有望在 4Q27 做小規模的量產，且
僅有 ANNAPURNA 及世芯有在台積電投片該晶片的權力。 
此次在法說會有提到，公司新增另外一個大客戶，公司未
透露細節，但非 GOOGLE，客戶為美國客戶，晶片就是與 AI
相關(未指出是推論還是訓練)，且該晶片在 27 年下半年
會貢獻營收，推估營收規模將達到 10 億美元。而世芯與該
客戶的生意將具有延續性。 
故綜合以上，世芯對於 28 年營收規模期待將有更大的跳
升，且 OPM 將會往 HIGH TEENS 邁進。

5. 26年約略維持預估，27年預估上修，28年粗估高度成長： 
3Q26世芯財報約略符合預期，雖然有供應鏈相關問題，不
過4Q26營收仍將續創新高，故維持26年營收預估為714.6
億元(原估737億元)，EPS 120.31元(原估120.3元)。展望
27年，CSP客戶三代晶片貢獻全年，加上爭取到的投片及訂
單配比上修，另外新增客戶也將貢獻營收，故上修營收為
1529.3億元(原估1335億元)，EPS上修至198.3元(原估
178.99元)。28年在CSP 4代晶片開始貢獻下，研究部粗估
1Q28小幅量產，2Q28開始大幅放量，推估28年營收將達到
2593億元，成長72.5%YOY，EPS預估再跳升至341.95元。

附錄一 ： 評 等 之 標 準 
評等 定義 
強力買進    預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25%以上 
買進 預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬 10%以上 
中立 預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 10～-10% 
降低持股 預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於(-10%)以下 
未評等 沒有足夠的基本資料判斷該公司評等

附 錄 二 ： 免 責 宣 言

告 中 所 有 的 意 見 及 預 估 ， 如 有 變 更 恕 不 另 行 通 知 。

本 研 究 報 告 所 載 之 投 資 資 訊 ， 僅 提 供 客 戶 做 為 一 般 投 資 參 考 ， 並 非 針 對 特 定 對 象 提
供 專 屬 之 投 資 建 議 。 文 中 所 載 資 訊 或 任 何 意 見 ， 不 構 成 任 何 買 賣 有 價 證 券 或 其 他 投
資 標 的 之 要 約 、 宣 傳 或 引 誘 等 事 項 。 對 於 本 投 資 報 告 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 任 何 證 券 、 投
資 標 的 ， 或 文 中 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 投 資 策 略 ， 投 資 人 應 就 其 是 否 適 合 本 身 財 務 狀 況 與
投 資 條 件 ， 進 一 步 諮 詢 財 務 顧 問 的 意 見 。 本 投 資 報 告 之 內 容 取 材 自 據 信 為 可 靠 之 資

陳 述 或 保 證 。 本 投 資 報 告 載 述 意 見 進 行 更 改 與 撤 回 並 不 另 行 通 知 。 本 投 資 報 告 並 非
（且不應解 釋 為 ）在 任 何 司 法 管 轄 區 內 ， 任 何 非 依 法 從 事 證 券 經 紀 或 交 易 之 人 士 或
公 司 ， 為 於 該 管 轄 區 內 從 事 證 券 經 紀 或 交 易 之 遊 說 。 本 投 資 報 告 內 容 屬 統 一 投 顧 之

### Page 1

www.imeritz.com

삼성전자 (005930)

Company Brief 
반도체/디스플레이 
Analyst 김선우 
sunwoo.kim@meritz.co.kr 
RA 우서현 
seohyun.woo@meritz.co.kr 2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정  
3Q26E 영업이익 106.1조원, 원화강세 불구 견조한 수준 기록 전망 
삼성전자의 3Q26 영업이익은 106.1조원으로 낮아진 시장 기대치 (104조원)를 소폭 
능가할 전망이다. 해당 분기동안 급격한 원화강세로 인해 실적 측면의 비우호적 환경
이 조성되었지만, 동사는 강건한 판매 정책에 기반해 메모리 판가가 산업 평균을 재
차 능가하며 견조한 실적 개선이 가능했다. 
동사 메모리 사업부의 3Q26 영업이익은 109조원으로 예상되며, 이중 DRAM과 
NAND는 각각 80조원 (OPM 78.5%)과 29조원 (OPM 68.9%)을 차지할 전망이다. 
DRAM과 NAND 출하량은 기존 예상치를 소폭 하회하는 +3.4%/+4.2% QoQ을 기
록하겠지만, 판가가 무려 +20.1%/19.0% QoQ 상승하며 실적 개선을 이끌겠다. 전
분기에 이어 ‘DS 특별경영성과급’ 충당금이 반영되며 손익에 일부 영향을 미치겠지
만, 동사 특유의 ‘수익성 극대화’ 판가 정책이 산업 우위 구도를 조성한다. 한편 LSI/
파운드리는 영업손실이 1.5조원을 기록하며 적자가 지속될 전망이다. 가동률 개선에 
기반한 사업 회복이 진행중이지만, 테일러팹 가동과 함께 비용 요인이 지속 출회할 
전망이다. 한편 반도체 외 기타 사업부의 험난한 실적 추이는 지속될 전망이다. SDC 
1.1조원 영업이익을 제외하고, DX (세트) 부문의 경우 MX -1.9조원, DA/VD -7,400
억원으로 영업적자 확대되리라 예상된다. 당사가 지난해 말부터 주장했듯, 세트 사업
은 메모리 등 부품원가 비중이 급격히 상승하는 가운데 향후 ‘판가 인상 → 판매량 
축소’의 ‘내구재화’ 변모 속 적자 최소화 노력만이 불가피할 전망이다.

1Q27 일부 모바일 DRAM 판가 +50% 폭등 예상. 적극적 매수 권고 
AGI를 향한 경쟁적이며 구조적 서버 수요 증가 불구, 스마트폰 중심의 B2C 응용처
는 유일한 판가저항처로 남아있다. 하지만 당사의 채널체크에 따르면, 메모리 업체들
의 지속적인 생산 전환 (LPDDR → 서버 DRAM 및 HBM)에 기반해 주요 고객 향 
모바일 메모리 판가는 무려 1Q27부터 50% 이상 QoQ 상향될 전망이다. 주요 고객
은 내년 20주년 플래그십 스마트폰을 제작하는 상황에서 판가 폭등을 감내해야하는 
상황이다. 최근 메모리 사이클 개선 속도에 대한 막연하며 진부한 우려가 존재하나, 
향후 1~2개월간 메모리 업체들에 대한 2027년 실적 추정치 상향과 함께 ‘성장·저평
가·주주환원 매력’이 동시에 발생할 전망이다. 동사의 2027년 영업이익 추정치를 기
존의 562조원에서 632조원으로 13% 상향조정하며, 시장 눈높이 역시 지속 상향되
리라 예상한다. 한편 배당 중심으로 발표됐던 동사의 주주환원의 경우, 모든 주주에게 
더 실효적인 ‘자사주 매입·소각’ 방안이 병행되는 방향으로 변모하리라 예상된다. 투
자의견 Buy와 적정주가 50만원을 유지한다. 
 Meritz Research  2026. 10. 7

Buy (20거래일 평균종가 대비 상승 여력 기준) 
적정주가 (12개월) 500,000원 
현재주가 (10.6) 272,000원 
상승여력 83.8%

Document 242: [207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 외형 성장 지속, 수익성 개선은 시간이 필요삼성바이오로직스(207940) 외형 성장 지속, 수익성 개선은 시_dcb09b7c

Source PDF: all report pdf/[207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 외형 성장 지속, 수익성 개선은 시간이 필요삼성바이오로직스(207940) 외형 성장 지속, 수익성 개선은 시_dcb09b7c.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-22
Pages: 9
Status: ok

### Page 1

www.imeritz.com

삼성바이오로직스 (207940)

Company Brief 
제약/바이오 
Analyst 김준영 
junyoung.kim@meritz.co.kr 
RA 김현비 
hyunbee.kim@meritz.co.kr 외형 성장 지속, 수익성 개선은 시간이 필요

3Q26 Review  
3Q26 연결 기준 매출액 1조 4,903억원(YoY +18.5%, QoQ +12.8%), 영업이익 
6,401억원(YoY +1.0%, QoQ +9.2%)으로 컨센서스를 각각 9.8%, 4.7% 상회했다. 
기존 1 ~ 4공장 풀가동 및 미국 Rockville 공장 매출 약 1,000억원과 5공장 PPQ 
물량 인식이 더해지며 외형 성장을 견인했다. 반면 신규 생산시설의 초기 비용 및 제
품 믹스 영향으로 OPM은 42.9%를 기록하며 전년 동기 대비 7.5%p 하락했다. 동
사는 연간 매출 성장률 가이던스를 기존 +15~20%에서 +20%로 상향했다. 당사는 
4분기 Rockville 공장 매출 기여와 기존 공장의 높은 가동률을 감안하여 연간 매출
액 5조 6,135억원(YoY +23.2%)을 예상한다.

신규 생산거점의 외형 확대, 이익 기여는 좀 더 긴 호흡으로 
5공장은 2Q26부터 PPQ 생산을 본격화했으나 상업 생산에 따른 실질적인 이익 기
여는 2027년 하반기에 확대될 전망이다. Rockville 공장은 3Q와 4Q 각각 약 
1,000억원의 매출이 예상되며 2027년 분기별 매출도 유사한 수준을 유지할 것으로 
전망한다. 다만 영업이익 기여까지는 시간이 더 소요될 것으로 예상되어 2027년 이
익 성장은 미국 공장보다 5공장 Ramp-up이 주도할 전망이다.

투자의견 Buy, 적정주가 200만원 유지 
삼성바이오로직스에 대해 투자의견 Buy, 적정주가 200만원을 유지한다. 고객사 수주 
문의가 회복되는 가운데 대형 계약 협의가 진행 중이며 6공장 역시 연말 또는 내년 
초 착공을 목표로 하고 있다. 다만 신규 수주가 실제 계약으로 이어지는 시점과 5공
장의 상업 생산 전환 속도가 중기 실적의 핵심 변수로 판단한다. 추가 대형 수주 확
보 시 5공장 이후의 성장 가시성도 높아질 것으로 예상한다. 
PolyPeptide 인수는 펩타이드 CDMO로 사업을 확장한다는 점에서 긍정적이다. 인
수 완료 이후 다음 분기부터 연결 매출 인식이 가능하나 유의미한 영업이익 기여까지
는 최소 2년 이상 소요될 전망이다. PolyPeptide 인수는 현재 추정치에서 반영하지 
않았으며 향후 인수 완료 시 연결 OPM 희석 가능성을 감안할 필요가 있다. 한편 최
근 법원 항고심에서 배양부터 정제까지의 생산공정이 쟁의행위 기간에도 정상 수행되
어야 할 보전작업으로 인정되면서 파업에 따른 생산 차질 우려는 완화될 것으로 판단
한다. 노사 협상은 여전히 진행 중이나 생산 안정성에 대한 고객사 우려가 낮아질 경
우 신규 수주에도 긍정적으로 작용할 전망이다. 
 Meritz Research  2026. 10. 22

Buy (20거래일 평균종가 대비 상승 여력 기준)

Document 258: _美元已死_？不，美元强势归来：DXY突破关键位，空头再遭重创

Source PDF: all report pdf/_美元已死_？不，美元强势归来：DXY突破关键位，空头再遭重创.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-13
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

美元指数突破关键技术位，升⾄ 2025 年 4 ⽉以来最⾼⽔平。美国经济表现、利率优势及外
资流⼊共同⽀撑美元，投机资⾦也持续加码多头。但过去⼀年创纪录的 1.47 万亿美元美国
资产投资，也可能成为美元的潜在⻛险：⼀旦美国资产吸引⼒下降或外汇对冲需求上升，
美元或⾯临回调压⼒。
摘要：
美元多头重新掌握主动权，看空美元的投资者再次落空。美元指数（ DXY ）近期突破关键技术
位，升⾄ 2025 年 4 ⽉以来最⾼⽔平。美国经济数据相对其他主要经济体表现较好，加之利率差异
仍对美元有利，为美元提供了⽀撑。
从技术⾯看，美元指数重新站上 200 周均线，与 2021 年底的⾛势呈现相似形态，且尚未出现明显
超买信号。与此同时，投机资⾦正在回流。据三菱⽇联⾦融集团（ MUFG ）数据，杠杆基⾦美元
多头头⼨已升⾄ 8 ⽉初以来最⾼⽔平，市场看涨情绪有所升温。
美元⾛强的另⼀⾯是欧元持续承压。欧元兑美元已跌⾄ 200 ⽇均线下⽅，初步下⾏⽬标指向
1.10 。不过，美元当前的重要⽀撑因素之⼀ —— 外资对美国资产创纪录的 1.47 万亿美元投资，也

可能成为未来最⼤的⻛险来源。⼀旦美国资产相对表现逆转，资本流⼊放缓或对冲需求上升，
都可能改变美元⾛势。
DXY 突破关键技术位，多头信号全⾯点亮
美元指数近期持续⾛强，最新突破将其推⾄ 2025 年 4 ⽉以来的最⾼⽔平。从技术指标看， DXY 已
重新站上 200 周均线， 200 ⽇均线则持续向上倾斜。相对强弱指数（ RSI ）尚未进⼊超买区域，
上⽅ 105 附近的主要趋势线构成下⼀道重要阻⼒，在此之前，美元仍有⼀定上⾏空间。
更⻓周期来看，⾃ 2011 年以来的美元主要上升趋势线再次提供⽀撑。据 LSEG Workspace 数
据，当前技术形态与 2021 年底⾼度相似。当时美元在类似的技术结构下开启了⼀轮显著升值⾏
情。
技术指标尚未发出明显的反转信号，也为美元多头继续押注提供了依据。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

经济表现差异与利率优势提供⽀撑
美元⾛强并⾮单纯由技术⾯推动，美国与其他主要经济体之间的增⻓表现差异，以及利率差
异，同样是重要⽀撑。
花旗经济意外指数显⽰，美国经济数据整体好于市场预期，但这更多反映的是相对于市场预期
的表现，并不意味着美国经济不存在增⻓压⼒。与此同时， MUFG 数据显⽰，美元⾛势与相对收
益率保持较⾼相关性。只要美国相对于其他经济体的利率优势没有明显收窄，美元就仍将获得
⽀撑。
欧元区的情况则相对不利。欧元区与美国之间的经济意外指数差距⼤幅收窄，反映出欧洲经济
数据相对表现偏弱，欧元兑美元也随之⾛低。
更令欧元承压的是，多项潜在利好均未能转化为汇率⽀撑。能源价格⼤幅下跌、欧洲央⾏释放
偏鹰派政策信号，都未能有效提振欧元，法国财政⻛险则进⼀步加重市场担忧。在相对经济表
现和利率差异均不利的情况下，欧元仍⾯临下⾏压⼒。
外资创纪录流⼊美国资产，为美元提供强⼒托底
除了经济和利率因素，外国资本持续流⼊美国资产，也构成美元的重要⽀撑。
据 MUFG 数据，截⾄今年 8 ⽉的⼀年内，外国投资者购买美国股票和企业债券的规模达到创纪录

的 1.47 万亿美元，接近全球⾦融危机前历史峰值的两倍。
由于外汇对冲⽐例仍处于低位，这些资⾦流⼊转化为直接的美元需求，进⼀步⽀撑美元⾛势。
美国资产的相对吸引⼒不仅影响美股表现，也对美元形成⽀撑。

### Page 3

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

写评论
图⽚
投机性持仓同样印证了这⼀趋势。 MUFG 最新持仓数据显⽰，杠杆基⾦的美元多头主要集中在做
多美元兑欧元和加元，其中欧元空头头⼨已升⾄ 2020 年 3 ⽉以来最极端⽔平之⼀。
这意味着，美元⾛强已不只是基本⾯因素推动的结果，投机资⾦也在进⼀步强化这⼀趋势。
1.47 万亿美元资本流⼊：最⼤⽀撑或成最⼤⻛险
不过，当前⽀撑美元的最强劲⼒量，未来也可能成为其最⼤的⻛险来源。
MUFG 警告，如果美国股市相对其他市场的表现持续逆转，外国投资者对美国资产的需求可能随
之降温，进⽽削弱美元的资本流⼊⽀撑。
值得注意的是，美元⾛弱并不⼀定需要外国投资者⼤规模抛售美国资产。即使他们继续持有美
股和企业债券，只要开始提⾼美元对冲⽐例，也可能显著改变外汇市场的供需关系，削弱此前

由资本流⼊带来的美元需求。
因此，下⼀轮美元趋势逆转的导⽕索，可能⾸先出现在美国股市，⽽⾮外汇市场本⾝。 MUFG 认
为，美国股市相对表现和外国投资者的持仓变化，将是判断美元强势能否持续的关键指标。
短期来看，技术⾯、利差和资⾦流向仍共同⽀持美元多头。但如果美国资产的相对吸引⼒开始
减弱，当前⽀撑美元的庞⼤资本流⼊，也可能逐渐转化为美元上涨周期的隐患。

Key points
• The Cotality home value index – covering
all of Australia – fell 1.2% in October
following a 1.3% decline in September
and 1.4% falls in August and July.

Annual growth has turned negative,
running at –2.8%yr with prices down
6.7% from their peak in March.
• Across the major cities, Brisbane and
Perth led the declines in the October
month, recording falls of 2.0% and
1.7% respectively, but the correction
remains more pronounced in Sydney
(prices down a further 1.6%mth to be
off 9.4%yr) with Melbourne also off
more sharply over the year (–7.6%yr)
but recording a milder October
(–0.8%mth). Adelaide remains relatively

resilient, recording a 1.2% decline in
the month but prices still up 3.7%yr.
• Preliminary estimates show the
number of sales, in rolling 3mth
terms, down 5.6%qtr and 22.5%yr.

2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
The Cotality home value index, covering all of Australia, fell
1.2% in October with slight revisions to previous months
again shaving a further 0.3% off prices. The correction
has now seen prices decline 6.7% from their March peak

with annual growth firmly in negative at –2.8%yr. Turnover
continues fall as well, down 22.5%yr. Housing-related
tax policy changes, interest rate rises and heightened
uncertainty have triggered a sharp contraction in markets.
The downturn is now comparable to the previous two
housing market corrections. Nationally, the downturn in
2022-23 saw prices decline 7.5% and turnover fall 29% peak
to trough. The 2017-18 correction saw prices fall 8.6% and

turnover by 24%, with larger falls in Sydney and Melbourne.
Note that unless specified, figures in titles, tables and
commentary are in non‑seasonally adjusted terms, consistent
with the ‘headline’ figures reported by Cotality. However,
figures shown in charts are in seasonally adjusted terms.
Preliminary estimates of turnover show rolling 3mth volumes
down 5.6%qtr in October, previous estimates again revised
lower. The monthly pattern suggests declines re‑accelerated

through September‑October after stabilising somewhat in July‑
August. The RBA's interest rate rise in late September and a
further expected tightening likely contributed to the shift.
The detail shows Brisbane and Perth are now leading the
way in terms of monthly price declines although corrections
to date remain significantly larger in Sydney and Melbourne
while smaller capitals and regional areas continue to see much
milder downturns. Across price tiers, all segments are now

showing similar rates of decline after top price tiers had seen
a more pronounced and earlier downturn. 'Top tier' segments
have tended to lead recent price cycles.
Looking across jurisdictions,
• Sydney prices dropped 1.6% in October having averaged
similar‑sized declines over the previous five months. While
all segments and tiers continue to record falls, the last few
months has seen the pace of decline moderate a touch in the
top tier segments that have led cycles in the past, although

they are still a long way from stabilisation.
• Melbourne prices declined 0.8%mth, in line with
September's move and continuing the slower pace of
retreat. As noted, this likely reflects the weaker starting
point coming into the correction, prices already at 5½ year
lows. Interestingly, the detail shows the moderation in price
declines in recent months has been more pronounced for
'top tier' units where prices are back where they were in
2016.
Housing downturn deepens

### Page 3

forecasts are based are reasonable, the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially
from these forecasts.
3WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
• Brisbane prices are tracking a very fast rate of decline
that is also seeing large downward revisions. Prices fall
2% in October with September and August now both
showing 2.1% falls, revised down from 1.5% and 1.6%

respectively (August having previously been estimated at
just 1%, July's move having seen a similar series of revisions
–1.2% to –1.8%). The combined effect has taken the rolling
3mth change to –6.1%qtr from –4.7%qtr last months and
–2.7%qtr in August and –0.6%qtr in July. At 0.4%yr, annual
price growth has virtually stalled flat having run at 18%yr as
recently as May. All tiers and segments are recording similar
rates of decline.
• Perth is seeing something very similar, prices dropping a

further 1.7% in October, with September's decline revised
down to 2.1% from –1.2% and August's to 2.0% from –1.7%.
At 3.9%yr, annual growth is still positive but off steeply
from the 24% pace back in May. As with Brisbane, all tiers
and segments are recording similar rates of decline.
• Adelaide dwelling prices posted a somewhat milder 1.2%
decline in October, , September's –1.3% decline marked
down to –1.7%. Annual price growth slowed to 3.7%yr,

similar to Perth's but with less of a whiplash from strong
gains to large declines. Turnover has also been much
steadier, sales holding flat over the last three months and
down just 12.4%yr.
• The smaller capitals were again more mixed, price growth
still positive in Darwin (0.6%mth, 9.7%yr), relatively resilient
in Hobart (+0.5%mth, 5.5%yr) but weaker in Canberra
(–0.7%mth, –3.7%yr).
• Regional markets showed a 0.9% decline in Qld but slightly
milder declines in NSW (–0.7%) and Victoria (0.6%). Annual

prices growth is in the 1.4‑3.5%yr range.
All up, the October updates make for bleak reading. With the
RBA widely expected to raise interest rates again, the near
term outlook remains very challenging. Next week's consumer
sentiment update will show how this is shaping housing‑
related sentiment – so far, consumer price expectations have
weakened but stopped short of swinging to the clear negative
consensus that emerged in the 2022‑23 correction. That now

looks to be at risk. Either way, momentum looks set to remain
weak into year‑end and, barring a significantly improved
inflation and interest rate outlook, well into 2027.

Document 324: Europe’s Deal Limiting Chinese Auto Imports (2026-11-02)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Europe’s Deal Limiting Chinese Auto Imports (2026-11-02).pdf
Report source: CFR (Follow the Money)
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 16
Status: ok

### Page 1

1
EXPERT TAKE FOLLOW THE MONEY
Europe’s Deal Limiting Chinese Auto Imports
Europe’s auto deal with China is a good start. But containing China’s export boom
will require a much broader response.
European Commissioner for Trade and Economic Security Maros Sefcovic speaks at a press briefing at the EU Delegation to
China in Beijing, China October 9, 2026. Florence Lo/Reuters
PUBLISHED
Articles Follow the Money Share
Log In
Regions Topics Research Experts Events The Future of American Strategy

### Page 2

2
November 2, 2026 11:02 a.m.
Brad W. Setser CFR EXPERT
Whitney Shepardson Senior Fellow
The year-over-year increase in Chinese automobile
exports to all markets is currently about 3.5 million
cars.
Over ten years, that is 35 million cars. The global
market excluding China is only around 60 million
cars. Japan raised its auto exports by about 3.5 million
cars in the 1970s—but that was over a decade, not
one year. And right now the U.S. and Indian markets

(around 20 million cars) are effectively closed to
Chinese production. The 2026 jump in exports is an
incredible.
China’s domestic market, though, is down 20 percent
this year—that works out to a decline of about 5
million cars. The Wall Street Journal estimates that
domestic sales will fall to around 19 million cars this
year, less than a third of the production capacity of
China’s auto industry.
So even though China’s exports are increasing at an

unprecedented pace, China’s domestic auto sector
isn’t really booming; production is essentially flat, and
intense domestic competition is destroying the
profitability of firms that don’t export.*

### Page 3

3
Europe’s new auto deal with China should be
understood in this context.
The deal limits China’s exports of hybrids (including
plug-in hybrids) to Europe to around 350,000 cars.
That is above the 300,000 hybrids that China
exported to Europe in 2025, but well below the (gulp)
800,000 annualized pace for hybrids in the last three
months of (European) data. Total imports,
annualized, were running at an incredible 2 million a
year pace thanks to strong battery electric imports in

September.

### Page 4

4
China appears to have concluded that it prefers a
guaranteed market for a limited number of cars (and
more or less guaranteed profits) to the risk of a
prohibitive tariff that would cut off all its exports.
And Europe preferred a negotiated deal to the risk of
retaliation and a slowdown in licensing rare earth and
magnet exports.
The Commission also thinks of this as a bit of a
model—more on that later.
I certainly understand the logic of the deal.

Something needed to be done. China’s export of
hybrids to Europe were up an insane 200 percent
year-over-year in the last 3 months of data, which led
to a 75 percent increase for all cars. One more year of
75 percent growth would bring China’s exports to

### Page 5

5
around 3 million cars; in two years, close to 6 million,
and in three years, 10 million—or most of the market.
But Europe still is a long way from a coherent trade
and industrial policy with respect to autos, let alone a
coherent economic policy towards overall trade with
China.
After this deal, as I understand it:
Hybrid imports are capped at 350,000, down
from a projected 800,000 in 2026—and then
rising to 370,000 and 390,000 in subsequent
years.

Battery electric vehicles face a tariff from 25
percent (Telsa, 10 percent base + 15 percent) to
45 percent (10 percent base plus 35 percent).
BYD’s total tariff is at 27 percent, which isn’t
prohibitive. Even with these tariffs, battery
electric imports will total around 600,000 this
year, up from 500,000 last year.
Diesel and gasoline (ICE) cars face a tariff of
10 percent. Europe is importing around
200,000 of these a year (including a significant

number from Chinese-owned MG).
There are no special tariffs on imported parts or
imported batteries, which matters for the growing
number of Chinese factories in Europe. So long as
tariffs are low and the renminbi is undervalued,
Chinese firms still have an incentive to maximize the

### Page 6

6
Chinese content of their “transplant” factories (ask
Thailand).
There is currently no unified European policy on
inward investment that requires joint ventures or
technology transfer either, only a lot of wishful
thinking.**
Sum it up, and it is a market where Chinese exports
are constrained by a hodge-podge of different trade
defense tools, and there is no standard policy for
ensuring that Chinese assembly operations use
European parts rather than import Chinese kits. The

existing operations, which are just starting to scale up,
don’t draw on a European supply chain. They likely
preserve a few assembly jobs at the expense of the
broader automotive ecosystem.
A coherent European (or national) industrial policy
for the automotive and battery sectors is thus still a
work in progress.
The safeguard on hybrids and the resulting tariff rate
quota only stops the bleeding.
And make no mistake, Europe’s position in the global

automotive sector is slipping. Europe still runs an
overall surplus in auto trade, but that surplus is falling
fast if trade in batteries is added to automotive trade
(there isn’t a split between batteries used for energy

### Page 7

7
storage and batteries used by cars in the EU data
right now, at least not that I have found).
Europe’s broader trade policy to China is similarly a
work in progress.
The surge in imports of hybrid cars was particularly
pronounced, but it is by no means the only such surge.
The balance in industrial machinery has eroded
significantly. Consider the following chart, which
shows imports and exports of machinery, electronics
and vehicles (HS 84, 85, and 87) by weight.

### Page 8

8
The balance in chemicals, measured by weight to
abstract from price changes, shows the same trend.
Europe doesn’t have an across-the-board tool for
applying tariffs against China like the U.S. section
301 statute.

### Page 9

9
But that isn’t necessarily an immediate constraint, as
there are plenty of solid safeguard cases thanks to the
current surge in European imports from China.
But if each safeguard needs to include a negotiated
tariff-rate quota that gives China enough guaranteed
market access (and Chinese firms enough guaranteed
profits) to ensure trade peace, Europe needs to get
moving with negotiation in other sectors.
Chemicals and machinery are more diverse sectors

than cars and thus aren’t as amenable to the same
high-level Š ef č ovi č -Wang negotiations the led to the
most recent deal.
This critique isn’t meant as criticism. Europe needed
to act on cars. Look at a chart of automotive trade in
kilos.

### Page 10

10
It’s just that it also needs to act in other sectors: auto
parts, batteries (though that is complicated),
chemicals, and some categories of industrial
machinery. The scale of China’s export boom and the
fact that in most sectors there are no limits to China’s
ability to scale up is a real problem.***
And Europe cannot wait for China to conclude that
it would rather grow on the basis of internal, rather
than external, demand.
I disagree with the IMF’s former top brass on this. A

China that grows on the back of a 1.5 pp net export
contribution for another couple of years will
deindustrialize much of the world. China’s trading
partners cannot step aside and do nothing until
China figures out how to escape deflation using its
own demand. Xi appears more than happy to
continue with his current policy mix and gamble that

### Page 11

11
the world will continue to absorb China’s ever
growing export surplus.
Pascal Lamy, Nicolas Köhler-Suzuki, and Victor Luiz
do Prado of the Jacques Delors Institute recognize
the need for a more systemic response—as do Jean-
Pierre Landau and Sébastien Jean, who have done an
excellent job explaining how China’s micro- and
macro-industrial policies fit together into a coherent,
self-reinforcing whole.
The proposals in the Lamy et al. paper overlap heavily

with the Commission’s current approach to China.
The authors envision a two-pronged approach: first,
negotiating with China to seek agreement on tariff-
rate quotas that effectively limit the EU’s imports
from China; and second, developing a broad yet
targeted tariff-based instrument to address structural
imbalances should negotiations fail. The latter would
give Europe a credible threat should China refuse to
agree to the proposed safeguards.
My own view: don’t let the perfect be the enemy of

the good.
The EU has shied away from developing its own 301
tool out of concern about preserving a “rules-based
system.” I still think a European 301 would allow a
more fulsome response, but understand that
European isn’t as willing as the United States to use

### Page 12

12
tools that at least appear to be outside those
authorized by the WTO. In reality the EU no longer
really wants to trade with China on the same (MFN)
terms applied to other WTO members. That mirrors
the current view of the United States. But the EU is
willing to go through more procedural hoops to get to
a similar result.****
But I also recognize that, absent policy adjustment in
China—notably a stronger currency that allows for
fair market competition—the scope of safeguards and

managed trade will progressively expand.
* One sign: Stellantis is resuming Jeep production in
China, but primarily for export rather than the
domestic market.
** There is a lot of (in my view) somewhat confused
analysis of Chinese investment that works off the
analogy provided by either foreign investment in
China’s auto sector or Japanese investment in the U.S.
and Europe in the 1980s. These are relevant examples
of course, but asserting that Chinese investment will

result in a similar outcome when the underlying
economics differ is a big logical jump. Japanese firms
had incentives to localize their parts production in the
United States from the then-strong yen as well as
from the risk of protection. Foreign firms operating in
China had to do JVs until recently and had incentives
to localize from China’s significant tariff on auto

### Page 13

13
parts and China’s lower cost structure (in part because
of a weak RMB). Tesla’s Shanghai incentives and tax
deal included strong localization incentives, and there
were additional incentives from Tesla’s desire to
qualify for the lists the governed access to China’s
consumer EV subsidies. That is why something like
90 percent of Tesla parts, including its batteries, are
now made in China. Chinese investors in Europe
have no similar incentives to localize right now, yet

many assume that the results will be the same. That,
to me, is naïve. Since the underlying economics differ
(Europe’s cost basis is much higher than existing
production in China) getting Chinese firms to do
more than final assembly in Europe will take stronger
policy tools than were required to get foreign firms to
localize parts production in China. Europe’s
Industrial Accelerator Act would be a step in the
right direction, but more is likely needed. Thailand

provides a useful cautionary example.
*** China can make over 60 million cars, according to
recent reporting from the Wall Street Journal. The
domestic market this year will be under 20 million
cars (down from something like 24 million cars last
year). Even with exports of 10 million, there is ~30
million cars worth of unused auto production
capacity (roughly double European demand). And
China is still investing at a pace that is consistent
with adding close to 5 million of EV production

capacity a year. Chinese firms, who don’t make any
money on domestic sales, are strongly incentivized to

### Page 14

14
This work represents the views solely of the author(s). The
Council on Foreign Relations is an independent,
nonpartisan membership organization, think tank, and
publisher, and takes no institutional positions on matters
of policy.
From Follow the Money and Greenberg Center for Geoeconomics
export. The world has never encountered such elastic
supply.
**** The more different the legal basis for the
European and U.S. response—and the more policy
differences (TRQs vs. a flat tariff, for example) the

harder it would be to ever develop a harmonized
policy approach. But with President Trump, a
harmonized policy isn’t even a U.S. goal.

Document 327: Fed Likely To Hike By 50bps In December

Source PDF: all report pdf/Fed Likely To Hike By 50bps In December.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-11-11
Pages: 7
Status: ok

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: Fed Likely To Hike By 50bps In December
Date:    7 hours ago
Fed Likely To Hike By 50bps In December

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
Fed Likely To Hike By 50bps In
December
By Ed Yardeni & Elias Griepentrog • 10 Nov 2026
View in browser

### Page 2

Photo by liam ward / Unsplash
I. The Fed Has More Work To Do
On October 26, we warned that the Fed could surprise markets with a
25bps rate hike at its October meeting. If the Fed chose to pass on doing
so because the midterm elections were too close, we predicted a 50bps
hike in December. Two days later, the FOMC voted 10-2 to hold the
federal funds rate (FFR) at 3.75%-4.00%, with the two dissenters favoring
a 25bps hike. Yet, in his October 28 presser, Fed Chair Kevin Warsh's

### Page 3

assessment of the economy sounded decidedly hawkish. Economic
growth was robust and broadening, the labor market was in good shape,
inflation remained above the Fed's 2% target, and financial conditions
were hardly tight.
So why did the Fed stand pat?
Warsh argued that financial conditions had tightened, inflation
expectations remained anchored, and stronger productivity growth was
allowing the economy to expand without overheating. With an information-

rich period ahead, the Fed saw little reason to rush. We called that
decision puzzling, comparing it to someone standing in a room with a fire
alarm blaring and smoke pouring under the door, yet insisting on more
evidence before calling the fire department.
Now Fed Governor Christopher Waller is sounding the same alarm.
Speaking in London on Tuesday, Waller said he would support a 25bps
rate hike in December even if inflation improved in October. However, if

October's core CPI increased by around 0.3% m/m or more, as it had in
the previous two months, he argued that a larger move would be
warranted. Specifically, he said that "a 50 basis point hike in the policy
rate should be considered at the December meeting." Waller warned that
moving by only 25bps under those circumstances would jeopardize the
Fed's inflation-fighting credibility.
Economic data released since Waller's remarks suggest that a 50bps hike

in December is becoming increasingly likely. We believe such a move is
warranted, as it would bring the FFR back to our estimated neutral range
of 4.25%-4.50%. Consider the following:
(1) CPI Inflation. October's headline CPI rose by 0.4% m/m, while core
CPI increased 0.3% m/m for the third consecutive month (chart).
Inflationary pressures remain broad-based, with particularly strong
increases in energy, food, and transportation services. Importantly, the

core CPI reading meets Waller's condition for necessitating a 50bps rate
hike in December.

### Page 4

Three forces are contributing to renewed goods inflation: the AI
investment boom, higher energy prices stemming from the Iran war, and
tariffs. In October, prices rose sharply in tariff-sensitive categories such as
furniture & bedding and appliances (chart). However, overall durable
goods prices were essentially unchanged, largely reflecting a 1.2%
decline in used car and truck prices.
Inflationary pressures were also widespread among nondurable goods,

with prices rising 0.5% m/m in October (chart). Recreational reading
materials, housekeeping supplies, footwear, and energy saw particularly
sharp increases. Apparel prices were essentially unchanged, while prices
for computer and software accessories fell.

### Page 5

Another concerning aspect in the inflation data is the persistence of
services inflation, reflecting resilient consumer demand, housing cost
pressures, and the pass-through of higher energy prices. Services prices
rose 0.4% m/m in October, including a 0.4% increase in services
excluding shelter (chart). Postage and delivery, hospital services,
transportation services, and motor vehicle insurance rose sharply.
Owners' equivalent rent also rose 0.3%.

(2) Small Business Sentiment. Echoing the CPI report's fire alarm,
October's NFIB survey showed that the net share of small business
owners planning to raise selling prices jumped to 37%, the highest since
July 2022, while 24% planned to raise worker compensation, the highest
since December 2025 (chart).

### Page 6

(3) Consumer spending. Spending remains remarkably robust. Redbook
same-store retail sales rose 10.1% y/y in the week of November 6, well
above the 2025 full-year average of 5.8% (chart).
(4) Labor market. One of the key reasons consumer spending remains
remarkably strong is the continued strength of the labor market. The latest
ADP data support this assessment. Private employers added an average
of 19,750 jobs per week during the four weeks ending October 24 (chart).

That translates into roughly 86,000 jobs per month, a healthy pace of job
creation that is sufficient to keep the unemployment rate near its current
low level of 4.1%.

### Page 7

clause.
Yardeni QuickTakes © 2026 – Unsubscribe

Document 354: Lipper - This Week in Earnings 26Q3

Source PDF: all report pdf/Lipper - This Week in Earnings 26Q3.pdf

### Page 1

PROPRIETARY RESEARCH FROM LSEG 
THIS WEEK IN EARNINGS

This Week in Earnings provides analysis and commentary on aggregate  
earnings estimate revisions, growth rates and valuations.

ANALYST: Tajinder Dhillon, CFA October 30, 2026 
tajinder.dhillon@lseg.com; 
THIS WEEK IN EARNINGS 
AGGREGATE ESTIMATES AND REVISIONS 
 26Q3 Y/Y earnings are expected to be 31.6%. Excluding the energy sector, the Y/Y earnings estimate is 26.4%. 
 Of the 299 companies in the S&P 500 that have reported earnings to date for 26Q3, 81.6% have reported earnings 
above analyst estimates. This compares to a long-term average of 67.7% and prior four quarter average of 81.5%. 
 26Q3 Y/Y revenue is expected to be 13.6%. Excluding the energy sector, the growth estimate is 12.2%. 
 69.9% of companies have reported 26Q3 revenue above analyst expectations. This compares to a long-term 
average of 63% and an average over the past four quarters of 76.8%. 
 For 26Q3, there have been 40 negative EPS preannouncements issued by S&P 500 corporations compared to 66 
positive EPS preannouncements. By dividing 40 by 66 the N/P ratio is 0.6 for the S&P 500 Index.  
 The forward four-quarter (26Q4– 27Q3) P/E ratio for the S&P 500 is 19.0. 
 During the week of Nov. 2, 144 S&P 500 companies are expected to report quarterly earnings. 
SECTION A: EARNINGS OUTLOOK 
Q3 2026: EARNINGS REVISIONS 
EXHIBIT 1A.  S&P 500: Q3 2026 SHARE-WEIGHTED EARNINGS ($B)

Source: LSEG I/B/E/S

### Page 2

PROPRIETARY RESEARCH FROM LSEG OCTOBER 30, 2026 
THIS WEEK IN EARNINGS 
This Week in Earnings provides analysis and commentary on aggregate earnings estimate revisions, growth rates and valuations.  2 
There has been an increase in the share-weighted earnings for the S&P 500 since the start of the quarter (to $778.4B 
from $760.7B). Three of the eleven sectors have experienced downward revisions to estimates.

Since Aug. 1, the communication services (-20.5%) and materials (-7%) sectors have recorded the highest percentage 
decreases in earnings, while the energy (33%) sector has recorded the highest percentage increase in earnings. 
Overall, share-weighted earnings for the S&P 500 have increased by 2.3% since the start of the quarter.

Since Aug. 1, the communication services (-14.7B) and materials (-1.2B) sectors have recorded the highest dollar-level 
decreases in earnings, while the energy (15.7B) sector has recorded the highest dollar-level increase in earnings. 
Overall, expected share-weighted earnings for the S&P 500 have increased by 17.7B since the start of the quarter.

The estimated earnings growth rate for the S&P 500 for 26Q3 is 31.6%. If the energy sector is excluded, the growth 
rate declines to 26.4%. The S&P 500 expects to see share-weighted earnings of $778.4B in 26Q3, compared to share-
weighted earnings of $591.4B (based on the year-ago earnings of the current 503 constituents) in 25Q3. 
11 of the 11 sectors in the index expect to see an improvement in earnings relative to 25Q3. The Energy and 
Information Technology sectors have the highest earnings growth rates for the quarter, while the Consumer 
Discretionary sector has the weakest anticipated growth compared to 25Q3. 
The Energy sector has the highest earnings growth rate (146.2%) of any sector. It is expected to earn $63B in 26Q3, 
compared to earnings of $25.6B in 25Q3. 4 of the 5 sub-industries in the sector are anticipated to see higher earnings 
than a year ago. The Oil & Gas Refining & Marketing (566.5%) and Integrated Oil & Gas (143.8%) sub-industries have 
the highest earnings growth in the sector. If these sub-industries are removed, the growth rate declines to 39%. 
The Information Technology sector has the second highest earnings growth rate (68.9%) of any sector. It is expected 
to earn $260.2B in 26Q3, compared to earnings of $154B in 25Q3. 12 of the 12 sub-industries in the sector are 
anticipated to see higher earnings than a year ago. The Semiconductors (137%) and Semiconductor Materials & 
Equipment (71.5%) sub-industries have the highest earnings growth in the sector. If these sub-industries are removed, 
the growth rate declines to 28.2%. 
The Consumer Discretionary sector has the lowest earnings growth rate (3.9%) of any sector. It is expected to earn 
$56.3B in 26Q3, compared to earnings of $54.2B in 25Q3. 7 of the 18 sub-industries in the sector are anticipated to 
see lower earnings than a year ago. The Casinos & Gaming (-44.1%) and Automotive Parts & Equipment (-38.8%) 
sub-industries have the lowest earnings growth in the sector. If these sub-industries are removed, the growth rate 
improves to 4.5%.

STARMINE EARNINGS SURPRISE FORECAST
Looking forward at quarterly performance, we use StarMine’s SmartEstimate® from LSEG to determine which
companies in the S&P 500 are better poised to beat and miss earnings estimates. The SmartEstimate® is a weighted

average of analyst estimates, with more weight given to more recent estimates and more accurate analysts. Our
studies have shown that when the SmartEstimate® differs significantly from the consensus (I/B/E/S mean estimate),
the Predicted Surprise accurately predicts the direction of earnings surprises or further revisions 70% of the time.
When a significant Predicted Surprise for revenue is also present for the period, the accuracy improves to 78%.

StarMine ARM from LSEG is an analyst revisions stock ranking model, designed to predict future changes in analyst
sentiment. ARM incorporates more accurate earnings estimates through the SmartEstimate prediction service. ARM
region rankings scores companies by region on a scale of 1 to 100 where 100 represents the most bullish sentiment.
Over the next two weeks, 147 S&P 500 companies are expected to report earnings. Of these companies, 17 positive

surprises and 11 negative surprises are expected from S&P 500 companies reporting quarterly results.

SOVEREIGN DEBT: WHEN SHORT-TERM SHOCKS AND SHIFTS IN DEBT STRUCTURE LEAVE A LASTING FISCAL
BURDEN — THE CASE OF BRAZIL
Salim Hammad
Brazil’s recent post-election yield shock risks leaving a lasting imprint on public finances — compounded by already high interest rates and a
changing debt structure. Large refinancing needs and a rising share of floating-rate debt will transmit rapidly and significantly higher interest

rates to the debt service through distinct but reinforcing channels.
SOURCES : MACROBOND, BCB

CHART 1
An abrupt post-election market awakening

In the wake of the 2 nd round of the general elections in late October, Brazilian markets — which had expected a Flavio Bolsonaro
victory — sold off sharply after Luiz Inacio Lula da Silva won a fourth presidential term. Both the equity and FX markets
experienced large intraday drops (14% of the Ibovespa stock index and -11% BRL vs. USD). Meanwhile, the yield on Brazil’s 10-year
government bond rose 250 basis points to 15.5%, reaching its highest level in over a decade (Chart 1) as investors worried about

the fiscal impact of costly campaign promises amidst large and persistent fiscal imbalances.
Fiscal risks have reshaped Brazil’s public debt structure, magnifying the cost of persistently high interest rates
Absent a confidence-infusing fiscal adjustment, Brazil’s interest bill is unlikely to decline in the short term, maintaining upward
pressure on public debt (83% of GDP in October 2026) and limiting budgetary leeway.
Three transmission channels, amplified by changing features of the debt structure, explain this persistence.

The first channel: The rise in long-term yields will increase rollover costs.
The sovereign must refinance roughly 20% of its debt stock each year. An average maturity of 4.1 years spreads refinancing over
time but does not shield the interest bill from a sustained rise in borrowing costs. As maturing securities are replaced, higher
issuance rates progressively lift the average borrowing cost — even for fixed-rate debt, whose interest costs remain unchanged

until refinancing. A sustained yield shock therefore feeds into debt servicing as the stock rolls over.

### Page 2

2
The bank for
a changing
world
Chart of the Week / 10 November 2026 economic-research.bnpparibas.com
The second channel: Demand for floating-rate instruments will strengthen until confidence in fiscal policy improves.
In turn, a larger share of SELIC-linked debt heightens the debt stock’s sensitivity to the prevailing high policy rate. Already SELIC-linked
securities reached a record 52.6% of the debt stock in August 2026, around 12 percentage points above their share at the start of President

Lula’s last term, versus a low of 18.4% in September 2014 (Chart 2). Weaker demand for fixed-rate securities with maturities exceeding five
years, amid increased fiscal uncertainty, has encouraged this shift 1.
The third channel: The BCB may delay rate cuts until fiscal policy over the next mandate is better defined.
A slower easing cycle would prolong high interest costs on SELIC-linked debt, whose servicing costs adjust with the policy rate without

waiting for refinancing. With these securities accounting for a growing share of the debt stock, delayed monetary easing would postpone
relief for the Treasury’s interest bill — even if long-term market yields subsequently ease.
These channels operate at different speeds: a rise in long-term yields increases rollover costs, whereas floating-rate debt reprices with
the SELIC rate, not automatically with 10-year yields. Meanwhile, a slower monetary easing cycle and stronger demand for floating-rate

securities compound pressure on the interest bill.
The fiscal–monetary policy nexus is therefore likely to remain a source of strain on Brazil’s public finances: fiscal uncertainty could delay
monetary easing, while greater floating-rate exposure would prolong the burden of high policy rates on the interest bill.
salim.hammad@bnpparibas.com
1 Fiscal slippage and uncertainty make investors less willing to lock in nominal returns over long horizons: if inflation or market yields rise, the purchasing power of fixed payments

declines or the bonds’ market value falls. Investors therefore demand higher yields to bear this risk, making long-term fixed-rate funding more expensive for the Treasury. By issuing
floating-rate bonds instead, the Treasury offers returns that adjust with the SELIC rate, sustaining demand, securing financing and reducing the risk of failed auctions. However
longer floating-rate maturities may reduce rollover risk, but do not remove the repricing exposure. Floating-rate issuance helps secure financing but transfers interest-rate risk to

the Treasury.
SOURCES : NATIONAL TREASURY, BNP PARIBAS

Document 469: インド株は長期の調整から一転して急反発、上昇基調は定着するか ～原油安と国際投資資金の回帰が追い風も、持続的な株高には企_72167bab

Source PDF: all report pdf/インド株は長期の調整から一転して急反発、上昇基調は定着するか ～原油安と国際投資資金の回帰が追い風も、持続的な株高には企_72167bab.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-11-11
Pages: 4
Status: ok

### Page 1

Asia Trends

2026 年11 月 11 日

1/4 
インド株は長期の調整から一転して急反発、上昇基調は定着するか 
～原油安と国際投資資金の回帰が追い風も、持続的な株高には企業業績の改善がカギに～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  西濵 徹 
（℡：050-5474-7495）

（要旨） 
○ 2026 年のインド株式市場は、イラン情勢の悪化に伴う原油高や世界的な金利上昇、ルピー安
などを背景に調整局面が続いた。また、世界的なＡＩ・半導体関連需要の拡大を受けて韓国
や台湾などに投資資金が集中する一方、インドのＩＴ関連企業はＡＩによる既存事業の代替
が懸念され、海外資金の流出が株価の重石となってきた。国内景気は底堅さを維持している
が、インフレ加速を受けたＲＢＩ（インド準備銀行）の金融引き締めも株価の上値を抑えた。 
○ しかし、米中間選挙を契機にイラン情勢の緊張緩和期待が高まり、原油価格が急落しており、
インド経済を取り巻く環境は好転している。さらに、米国でのデータセンター規制強化への
警戒からＡＩ・半導体関連銘柄に売り圧力がかかり、国際投資家による投資資金の再配分が
進み、インド株への資金回帰が株価の反発を促している。企業業績の底堅さや株価の割高感
の後退に加え、ＡＩ導入支援など新たな収益機会への期待も株価を押し上げている。 
○ 先行きについては、原油価格の落ち着きや海外投資資金の流入が続けば、株価の上昇基調が
強まる可能性がある。一方で、イラン情勢の再悪化による原油価格の反発やインフレ圧力の
長期化、金融引き締めに伴う景気下押しリスクには注意が必要となる。このところの株価の
急反発の動きは、外部環境の好転と投資資金の再配分による影響が大きく、上昇基調を定着
させるためには、企業収益の持続的な拡大が不可欠と考えられる。

### Page 2

2/4 
【市場環境の好転を受けて、調整局面が続いたインド株は一転して急上昇している】 
このところのインド株式市場では、主
要株価指数（ムンバイ SENSEX）が急速に
上昇しており、足元では約９ヵ月ぶりの
高値を回復している（図１）。長期にわた
る調整局面から一転して買いが強まっ
ているものの、依然として 2025 年末の
水準を下回っている。足元の株式相場の
急反発の背景には、海外投資資金の流入
が大きく影響している。先行きも相場が
上昇基調を強めることができるか、その
持続性を見極める必要がある。

【2026 年のインド株式市場が調整の動きを強めてきた背景とは】 
2026 年のインド株式市場は調整局面が続いてきた。背景には、イラン情勢の悪化による原油価格
の上昇に加え、米国をはじめとする主要国での長期金利上昇、通貨ルピー安など、外部環境の悪化
が重なったことがある。インドは、原油需要の大部分を輸入に依存しており、原油高は貿易赤字の
拡大やインフレ圧力の高まりを通じて、景気や企業業績に悪影響を及ぼしやすい構造にある。また、
インフレ懸念の高まりは金融引き締め観測を強め、株式市場のバリュエーションを圧迫する要因と
なった。国際金融市場では、世界的なＡＩ（人工知能）・半導体関連需要拡大を追い風に、関連産業
の割合が高い韓国や台湾などに資金が集中する動きがみられた。インド株式市場にも多くのＩＴ
（情報通信）関連企業が上場しているものの、その多くはＩＴサービスやソフトウェア関連事業を
主力としており、ＡＩの普及によって既存業務が代替されるとの懸念から、ＡＩ・半導体関連需要
の拡大による恩恵を受けにくいとみなされてきた。さらに、ＡＩの爆発的な普及により、英語が広
く使用される同国の言語面での優位性が薄れるとの見方も、投資対象としての魅力を低下させた。
その結果、海外投資家によるインド株式の売却が進み、株価の下押し圧力となってきた。 
金融市場は調整の動きを強めたものの、インド経済が大幅に悪化していた訳ではない。４-６月
の実質ＧＤＰ成長率は前年比＋7.8％と高い伸びを維持しており、個人消費をはじめとする内需を
けん引役にした底堅い動きが確認されている（注１）。一方で、９月のインフレ率は前年比＋5.54％
と前月（同＋4.83％）から加速しており（図２）、４ヵ月連続でＲＢＩ（インド準備銀行）が定める
目標レンジ（４±２％）の中央値を上回り、2024 年 10 月以来の高い伸びとなった。同国では 2025

### Page 3

3/4 
年９月、トランプ米政権が実施した関税
引き上げによる悪影響の緩和を目的に、
ＧＳＴ（財・サービス税）の実質引き下
げに動き、インフレ率が下振れした。こ
のため、先行きのインフレ率はその反動
により加速しやすい状況にある。さら
に、ルピー安による輸入物価の押し上げ
がインフレ圧力を増幅させる可能性も
高まっている。このため、ＲＢＩは 10 月
の定例会合で３年８ヵ月ぶりの利上げ
に舵を切った（注２）。さらに、先行きの
政策姿勢を「慎重な金融引き締め」に変更するなど、追加利上げの可能性を残した。このため、国
内景気の底堅さが株式市場にとって追い風となる一方、物価高と金融引き締めが株価の上値を抑え
る構図が続いてきた。

【米中間選挙を受けたイラン情勢の好転期待、世界的な投資資金の変化も追い風に】 
こうした状況が変化するきっかけとなったのが、11 月３日に実施された米国の中間選挙の結果
を受けた国際金融市場を取り巻く環境の変化であろう。トランプ米大統領がイランに対する追加的
な軍事行動に慎重な姿勢を示したことを受けて、イラン情勢の緊張緩和に対する期待が高まり、原
油価格は急速に下落している。その結果、株価の重石となってきたマクロ経済環境の悪化に対する
懸念が後退して、投資家のリスク許容度を高める要因となっている。 
国際金融市場における世界的な投資資
金の動きにも変化が生じている。米国で
は、中間選挙を経て、連邦議会上下両院
を民主党が掌握する『分割政府』となる
ことが決定している。これを受けて、米
国では今後、データセンター関連投資に
対する規制強化やＡＩ関連設備投資の伸
び鈍化を警戒する動きが広がっている。
その結果、これまで株価上昇をけん引し
てきたＡＩ・半導体関連銘柄に売り圧力
がかかっている。国際投資家の間では、
ＡＩ関連銘柄に集中した投資ポートフォリオの見直しが進んでおり、上述のように内需をけん引役

### Page 4

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
4/4 
にした経済成長が続くインド株式へ資金を振り向ける動きが出ている。こうした資金回帰は株価上
昇を加速させるとともに、ＲＢＩが 10 月にルピー相場の防衛策を相次いで導入したことも重なり
（注３）、その後のルピー相場の安定にも寄与しているとみられる（図３）。

【インド企業への見方の改善も株価を後押する一方、先行きは上下双方に振れるリスクは残る】 
インド企業の業績を巡る見方にも改善がみられる。足元では、銀行や金融、消費関連企業などを
中心に底堅い業績が確認されており、年明け以降の株価調整によって割高感が和らいできたことも、
投資資金の流入を後押ししている。また、ＡＩによって既存事業が代替されるとの懸念が意識され
てきたＩＴ関連企業に対しては、企業の既存システムや業務プロセスへのＡＩ導入に関わる「ＡＩ
インテグレーション業務」やＡＩ導入支援サービスの拡大が、新たな収益機会として期待されるよ
うになっている。さらに、同国には多数のＧＣＣ（グローバル・ケイパビリティ・センター）が稼
働しており、生成ＡＩを活用した業務改革・改善を加速させる動きがみられる。年明け以降の株価
調整を受けて SENSEX の株価収益倍率（ＰＥＲ）は低下しており、足元でも過去 10 年間の平均的な
水準を下回るとされる。そのため、先行きも業績拡大が続けば株価の再評価が進む余地は残されて
いる。海外の主要証券会社の間でもインド株に対する投資判断を引き上げる動きがみられる。 
先行きのインド株式市場については、原油価格の落ち着きが続き、海外投資資金の流入が持続す
れば、底堅く推移する可能性がある。企業業績の改善や国内の信用需要の拡大が確認されれば、海
外資金の買い戻しに加え、企業収益の拡大を背景とする本格的な上昇局面へ移行する余地が生まれ
る。一方で、イラン情勢を巡っては、米国とイランの協議が前進する兆しはみられるものの、包括
的な合意には依然として距離があるとされ、ホルムズ海峡やバブ・エル・マンデブ海峡などを取り
巻く環境には不透明感が残る。このため、原油価格が再び上昇に転じる可能性はくすぶる。加えて、
当面のインフレ率上振れ懸念からＲＢＩは金融引き締め姿勢を維持しており、インフレ圧力が十分
に沈静化しなければ、金利高が国内景気や企業業績の重石となる懸念も残る。 
このところのインド株の急反発は、これまでの下落をもたらしてきた外部環境が好転したことに
加え、国際投資資金の再配分が急速に進んだことによる影響が大きい。このため、短期的にみれば、
急ピッチの上昇に伴う利益確定売りの動きなどを通じて値動きが不安定化する可能性は残る。一方
で、中期的にみれば、イラン情勢の行方による原油価格の動向、金融引き締め圧力の行方、企業業
績の動向が相場の方向性を左右すると見込まれる。インド株式市場は調整局面からの脱却に向けた
重要な局面を迎えているものの、上昇基調の定着には海外資金の回帰だけでなく、企業収益の持続
的な拡大が不可欠であると考えられる。 
以 上

注３ 10 月15 日付レポート「インド中銀、ルピー防衛に向けて異例の措置を相次ぎ導入」

十一月 09, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  Powered by the EFA Platform 1

動態更新 PC零組件 ‧ 台灣

新普科技 
(6121.TW/6121 TT)

近期筆電展望疲弱將限縮獲利成長 
重要訊息 
毛利率與營業利益率雙雙不如預期導致新普 3Q26 每股盈餘僅 6.56 元，落
後凱基預估。我們預期 4Q26 至 2027 年非 IT 業務營收將持續成長，動能主
要來自電池備援模組 (BBU)，帶動獲利率與淨利成長。 
評論及分析 
3Q26 獲利不如預期。3Q26 EPS 為 6.56 元，較凱基預估低 25%，主因 IT 
與非 IT 毛利率均低於預期。3Q26 合併毛利率季減 2.0 個百分點至 
16.3%，其中 IT 毛利率因營收不振與成本壓力季減 2.9 個百分點至 9.2%，
非 IT 毛利率季減 2.8 個百分點至 36.3%，因子公司 AES-KY (6781 TT ，
NT$736，增加持股) 的 BBU 銷售組合不利。3Q26 營利率亦季減至 9.7%，
其中 IT、非 IT 營利率分別降至 4.4%、24.6%。3Q26 IT 營收占比縮減至 
73.7%，非 IT 則升至 26.3%。雖有匯兌收益抵銷，3Q26 EPS 仍低於凱基預
估及市場共識，累計 1Q-3Q26 EPS 為 22.45 元，年增 6%。新普並通過以 
1H26 獲利發放現金股利 11.28 元，股利發放率為 71%。 
非 IT BBU 產品將帶動 2027年營收擴張。新普 10 月營收 56.1 億元，月減 
7%、年減 29%，其中含 AES-KY 營收則月增 1%、年增 23%，顯示非 IT 
營收強勁，而筆電需求依舊乏力。新普預期 4Q26 IT 營收將下滑，主因淡季
效應，以及 iPhone 需求疲弱、良率不如預期，而 AES-KY 看好 4Q26 營
收。我們預期新普 4Q26 非 IT 營收占比將擴大，不過下修 4Q26 與 2026 
年 EPS 預估，以反映毛利率與營利率低於預期。凱基將 2026 年 EPS 預估調
降至 28.93 元，年減 6%。展望 2027 年，因零組件成本上升，筆電需求將
持續下滑，可能要到 2H27 才有機會回升，而 BBU 與輕型電動車 (LEV) 營收
明年有望成長。因此，來自 AES-KY 的非 IT 營收將持續成長，占比將升至 
34%，年增 30%，對比 2026 年分別為 26% 與 25%，凱基預期資料中心
伺服器需求成長將推升 BBU 營收，加上 2027 年 AI 伺服器功率擴增。因產
品組合改善，加上 AES-KY 以外的其他非 IT 營收擴張，其毛利率優於筆電
及公司平均，將使新普明年毛利率將改善。然而，為反映 IT 營收預估下
修，下修 2027 年 EPS 預估至 29.96 元，年增 4%。 
投資建議 
有鑑於營收、獲利展望不如預期，我們將目標價調降至 390 元，隱含 13 倍
2027 年本益比。凱基認為新普短期內營收、獲利率回升動能有限，故儘管
其現金殖利率約 6%頗為誘人，我們仍將投資評等調降為「持有」。 
投資風險 
成本攀升下消費性產品需求存在不確定性、新台幣升值。

2. 儘管非 IT 業務成長，但 IT 營收衰退將使
4Q26 整體營收季減。2027 年營收成長仍
將仰賴非 IT 業務 (BBU 與 LEV 營收反彈)。 
3. 2027 年非 IT 營收成長將為獲利率與每股
盈餘提供支撐，而儘管現金殖利率誘人，
但我們認為新普短期的股價利多有限。

新普成立於 1992 年，生產筆電電池模組為主，為全球第一大電
池模組廠，近 10 年陸續增加電池模組供應之應用領域至平板、
手機、穿戴裝置，以及儲能。客戶包括全球前六大筆電廠：惠普
（美）、戴爾（美）、宏碁(2353 TT，NT$31，未評等）與蘋果
（美）。新普與日本新力、大陸德賽和欣旺達、以及台灣的順達
(3211 TT，NT$253.5，未評等）、加百裕（3323 TT，NT$24.9，
未評等) 等電池模組廠在不同領域直接競爭。新普目前在常熟與
重慶擁有電池模組的生產基地。

ESG 圖表定義 
項目 定義 說明 
能源使用 
直接與間接能源消耗總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司運營範圍內消耗之能源總量 
- 能源使用總量 = 直接能源消耗總量 + 間接能源消耗量 
- 總能源使用量包括購買的能源、生產的能源 
- 就公用事業而言，輸電/電網損耗為其業務活動之一部分，被視為總能源消耗，數據不包括
為滿足能源使用而生產的電力（公用事業為出售而生產） 
- 就公用事業而言，用於能源生產的煤、天然氣或核能等原料不屬於「總能源使用」項下

購買之再生能源 
Primary 再生能源購買總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司各種來源之能源消耗量與購買的能源中屬於自然界可再生者（太陽能、風能、水能、
生物質能、地熱能）之量  
- 如無證據顯示再生能源由公司生產，則所報告的能源數據視為購買的再生能源

再生能源使用率 再生能源占總能源消耗量之比例  
CO2 約當排放量 
直接 CO2 與 CO2 約當排放量(公噸) 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量（範圍 1 排放量） 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳 (CO2)、甲烷 (CH4)、一氧化二氮 (N2O)、氫氟碳化物 (HFCS)、全氟化
合物 (PFCS)、六氟化硫 (SF6)、三氟化氮 (NF3)

CO2 約當排放量營收比 
直接 CO2 與 CO2 約當排放量（公噸）銷售（百萬元）比 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量（範圍 1 排放量） 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳 (CO2)、甲烷 (CH4)、一氧化二氮 (N2O)、氫氟碳化物 (HFCS)、全氟化
合物 (PFCS)、六氟化硫 (SF6)、三氟化氮 (NF3)

廢棄物總量 
廢棄物總量(公噸) 
- 總廢棄物 = 無害廢棄物 + 有害廢棄物 
- 僅考慮固體廢棄物，但如液體廢棄物以公噸為單位呈報，則會將其加入求得包含液體廢棄
物之總量 
- 對於採礦、石油與天然氣等行業，tailings、廢石、煤、飛灰等廢棄物亦考慮在內

廢棄物回收率 
公司呈報的廢棄物回收率 
- 廢棄物回收率 = 廢棄物回收量/總廢棄物*100 
- 廢棄物轉化為能源或經由廢棄物焚燒產生能源視為廢棄物回收 
- 經由堆肥回收的廢棄物視為回收之廢棄物

總取水量 
總取水量(立方公尺) 
- 由呈報組織直接或經水公司等中介機構從任何水源抽取的總水量 
- 水井、城鎮/公用事業/市政用水、河水、地表水等不同之水源均予以考慮

環保支出 
環保支出總金額 
- 所有用於環境保護，或防止、減少、控制環境因素、影響、危害的投資與支出，亦包括處
置、處理、衛生、清理支出

員工流動率 
員工流動率 
- 包括任何原因（自願或非自願）離開公司的員工，如辭職、退休、自然離職/死亡、醫療失
能、冗員、裁員、重組、解僱、裁減或定期合約到期 
- 員工流動率 =（離開之員工/平均員工人數）*100 
- 平均員工人數 =（本年末員工人數 + 去年末員工人數）/2 
- 本財年末員工數 =上個財年末員工數 + 新員工數 - 離開之員工數

女性經理 
女性經理百分比 
- 女性經理占公司經理的百分比 
- 如有不同階層之百分比，如最高階、高階、中階、初階，則會考慮中階女性經理之百分比 
- 女性經理百分比 = 女性經理人數/經理總數*100

女性員工 
女性員工百分比 
- 女性員工占公司員工總數的百分比 
- 女性員工百分比 = 女性人數/員工總數*100

教育訓練總時數 
所有員工教育訓練總時數 
- 僅考慮員工教育訓練時數 
- 包括一般員工所有類型的教育訓練（如健康與安全、環境、急難事件救援、技能與職業發
展） 
- 如果數據以天為單位，則乘以 8，係假設 1 天 =  8 小時工作

每位員工教育訓練時數 平均每年每位員工總教育訓練時數  
資料來源：Refinitiv；凱基

台北富邦銀行

匯率市場總評：美元漲勢將暫緩
投資研究部 
 Research 
Financial Market 
▎全球最大淨負債國，美元居主導地位 
美國 2026 年第 2 季國際投資部位(IIP)顯示，美國對外
資產 47 兆、負債 69.4 兆美元，淨國際投資部位(NIIP)
為負 22.4 兆美元，居全球最大淨負債國。而負債主要
集中於證券投資項，佔整體淨負債近 9 成，外資持有美
股及美債規模達 40.9 兆，全球資金持續流向美元資產。

美國直接投資部位接近平衡，證券投資方面，美國持有
高報酬海外股權與直接投資，分別佔資產比重約 35.8%、

圖 1 美國主要持有外國股票、直接投資

資料來源：2026/9/14，Bloomberg、投資研究部

32.4%(見圖 1)，對外負債則以股票及債券、直接投資為
主(見圖 2)，今年第 2 季投資組合淨部位為負 19.9 兆美
元(見圖 4)，全球資金持續配置美國金融資產，美股、
美債市場規模穩居全球之首，美元主導地位難以被撼動。

圖 2 外國主要持有美國股票、債券

資料來源：2026/9/14，Bloomberg、投資研究部

美國直接投資呈現雙向擴張趨勢，今年第 2 季美國對外
直接投資高達 1515 億美元，顯示美國企業在 AI 供應
鏈重組與全球產能布局需求下，再度加速海外投資，不

◼ 美國居全球最大淨負債國，證券投資淨負債佔比達 9 成，外資持有美股及美債規
模達 40.9 兆，全球資金除了流向美元資產，AI 資料中心與先進製造業擴廠潮帶動
下，全球資本也朝美國集中，今年第 2 季外國對美直接投資達 1873 億美元。

◼ 今年 9 月升息後，美國 10 年公債利率與政策利率差值擴大，市場預期美國經濟
仍具韌性，期中選舉後迎來消費旺季，年底前資金可望流入具獲利、估值相對合
理的新興亞股，美元將受到抑制，不過升息預期猶存下，美元將不時回升。 
◼ 雖然 AI 產業需求強勁，不過新雲端服務業者面臨籌資難度加大問題，隨著資金成
本提升，市場將更謹慎關注 AI 產業的高成長、能否順利轉化為自由現金流，一旦
科技巨頭資本支出放緩、企業投資變現速度不如預期，或新創企業融資斷鏈等，
多重風險恐加劇美股震盪，預計避險情緒升溫將驅動美元走強。

誠信親切、專業創新 週報

圖 3 2026/2Q 美國居 NIIP 最大淨負債國 
資料來源：2026/10/1，Bloomberg、投資研究部

圖 4 外資持續投入美國金融資產

資料來源：2026/10/5，Bloomberg、投資研究部

圖 5 美國直接投資呈雙向擴張

資料來源：2026/10/27，Bloomberg、投資研究部

過美國金融帳對外直接投資負債也達 1873 億美元，創
近年高點(見圖 5)，包含來自台積電、三星等半導體投
資，AI 資料中心、電池與先進製造業擴廠潮，加上美國
政府推動製造業回流，全球資本也持續向美國集中，今
年前 2 季直接投資淨流入美國，中長期將支撐美元。

▎景氣仍具韌性，美德利差將支撐美元  
10 月 FOMC 維持政策利率，通膨仍然偏高，聯準會需
累積更多資訊，以判斷適當的貨幣政策，確保通膨及時
回到 2％，10/29 市場預期 12 月將升息 1 碼機率約
84%，明年將再升息 2 碼，自今年 9 月升息以來將累計
升息 4 碼，預計將支撐美國 10 年公債利率於高檔波動。

自 1990 年以來，美國 10 年公債利率與 Fed Fund Rate
差值處於負 1.6%到 4%區間(見圖 6)，而在步入升息循
環後，兩者差值大多呈現縮窄，包含 1994/2、2004/6、
2016/12、2022/3，反映持續升息對景氣具抑制效果。

1999/6 啟動升息循環後，美 10 年公債利率與政策利
率差值持續擴大、於 2000/1 才自波段高位反轉向下，
當時網際網路革命帶動生產力大幅提升，企業投資與股
市熱絡，市場預期未來經濟將高速成長，S&P 500 指數
持續創高、直到 2000/3/24 網路泡沫才破滅。而今年 9
月升息後，美 10 年公債利率與政策利率差值擴大，反
映目前政策利率環境下，市場預期美國經濟仍具韌性。

圖 6 近期美 10 年公債利率、政策利率差值擴大  
資料來源：2026/10/13，Bloomberg、投資研究部

近期美德實質利差自高檔回落抑制美元(見圖 7)，不過
歐元區景氣相對美國疲弱，此外，法國政府面臨預算協
商壓力，由於國會呈現分裂，政府難以推動數百億歐元
的財政整頓計畫，市場擔憂法國赤字與債務問題恐持續
惡化，帶動法國公債利率上升、法德公債利差擴大，市
場對歐元區財政紀律信心轉弱，對歐元形成壓力，而明
年 4 月法國將迎來總統大選，政治不確定性升溫，將可
能進一步抑制歐元。

圖 7 美德實質利差自波段高位回落

資料來源：2026/10/29，Bloomberg、投資研究部

▎資金成本升溫，美股震盪將支撐美元 
英特爾、博通執行長不約而同指出，資金取得管道可能
超越晶片供應，成為限制 AI 擴張的重大因素，而輝達
黃仁勳也表示，AI 產業需求強勁，新雲端服務業者
(neocloud)資金短缺、籌資難度加大。近期 CoreWeave
債券價格、股價雙雙暴跌(見圖 8)，反映出市場對其高
槓桿擴張模式的疑慮，隨著資金成本提升，市場將更謹
慎關注 AI 產業的高成長、能否順利轉化為自由現金流， 
AI 新創 Anthropic 將於 11 月中下旬上市，儘管第 3 季
營收達 168 億美元，不過季增率由從 147%放緩至 45%，
市場對其 1.5 兆到 2 兆美元 IPO 估值保持高度警覺。
